Implement the following plan:

# Top-level Semantic Releases Page

## Context
The dashboard "View all" link for semantic releases currently points to `/projects/[id]/semantic-releases`, a per-project page. We want a top-level `/semantic-releases` page (like the existing `/releases` page) that shows all semantic releases across projects with a project filter dropdown. The dashboard "View all" links to `/semantic-releases?project={id}`. The semantic release detail page breadcrumb also needs updating to link back to `/semantic-releases`.

## Changes

### 1. Backend: Add `ListAllSemanticReleases` endpoint

**`internal/api/semantic_releases.go`** — Add `ListAllSemanticReleases` to the store interface and a `ListAll` handler method
- Add `ListAllSemanticReleases(ctx, page, perPage) ([]models.SemanticRelease, int, error)` to `SemanticReleasesStore` interface
- Add `ListAll` handler: `GET /api/v1/semantic-releases` → calls `ListAllSemanticReleases()`
- Pattern: copy from `releases.go` `List` handler

**`internal/api/pgstore.go`** — Implement `ListAllSemanticReleases`
- `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM semantic_releases` for total
- `SELECT ... FROM semantic_releases ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT $1 OFFSET $2`
- Pattern: copy from existing `ListSemanticReleases`, remove `WHERE project_id = $1`

**`internal/api/server.go`** — Register the new route
- Add `mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/semantic-releases", chain(http.HandlerFunc(semanticReleases.ListAll)))` before the `{id}` routes

**`internal/api/semantic_releases_test.go`** — Update mock + add test
- Add `ListAllSemanticReleases` to `mockSemanticReleasesStore`
- Register `GET /semantic-releases` in `setupSemanticReleasesMux`
- Add `TestSemanticReleasesHandlerListAll` test

### 2. Frontend API client: Add `listAll`

**`web/lib/api/client.ts`** — Add to `semanticReleases` object:
```ts
listAll: (page = 1, perPage = 25) =>
  request<ApiResponse<SemanticRelease[]>>(`/semantic-releases?page=${page}&per_page=${perPage}`),
```

### 3. New top-level `/semantic-releases` page (mirrors `/releases`)

**`web/app/semantic-releases/page.tsx`** — New route page (thin wrapper)

**`web/components/semantic-releases/semantic-releases-list.tsx`** — Rewrite to match releases page pattern:
- Accept optional `initialProject` from `?project=` query param (via `useSearchParams`)
- Project filter dropdown (fetch all projects, "All Projects" default)
- Two SWR calls: `srApi.listAll()` for all, `srApi.list(projectId)` for filtered
- Table or card rows with: project name, version (VersionChip), status (colored text), urgency chip, age, delete button
- Pagination (Previous/Next, N–M of total)
- Empty state: centered Fraunces italic
- No back link (it's a top-level page now)

### 4. Update dashboard "View all" link

**`web/app/projects/page.tsx`** — Change link:
- From: `/projects/${projectId}/semantic-releases`
- To: `/semantic-releases?project=${projectId}`

### 5. Update semantic release detail page breadcrumb

**`web/components/semantic-releases/semantic-release-detail.tsx`** — Update breadcrumb and delete redirect:
- "Semantic Releases" breadcrumb link: `/semantic-releases?project=${projectId}` (instead of `/projects/${projectId}/semantic-releases`)
- `handleDelete` redirect: `/semantic-releases?project=${projectId}` (instead of `/projects/${projectId}/semantic-releases`)

### 6. Add sidebar nav entry

**`web/components/layout/sidebar.tsx`** — Add nav item:
- `{ href: "/semantic-releases", label: "Semantic Releases", icon: Brain }` after Releases
- Import `Brain` from lucide-react

### 7. Delete old per-project route

**`web/app/projects/[id]/semantic-releases/page.tsx`** — Delete this file
- The detail route `web/app/projects/[id]/semantic-releases/[srId]/page.tsx` stays (linked from agent runs)

## Files Modified
1. `internal/api/semantic_releases.go` — interface + ListAll handler
2. `internal/api/pgstore.go` — ListAllSemanticReleases implementation
3. `internal/api/server.go` — register GET /api/v1/semantic-releases
4. `internal/api/semantic_releases_test.go` — mock + test for ListAll
5. `web/lib/api/client.ts` — add `listAll` method
6. `web/app/semantic-releases/page.tsx` — new route page
7. `web/components/semantic-releases/semantic-releases-list.tsx` — full rewrite
8. `web/app/projects/page.tsx` — update "View all" link
9. `web/components/semantic-releases/semantic-release-detail.tsx` — update breadcrumb + delete redirect
10. `web/components/layout/sidebar.tsx` — add nav entry
11. `web/app/projects/[id]/semantic-releases/page.tsx` — delete

## Verification
- `go test ./internal/api/...` — all tests pass including new ListAll test
- `npx tsc --noEmit` — no type errors
- Dashboard "View all" → `/semantic-releases?project=...` → shows filtered list
- Filter dropdown → "All Projects" shows everything, specific project filters correctly
- Pagination works
- Delete button works with confirmation dialog
- Click a row → detail page, breadcrumb links back to `/semantic-releases?project=...`
- Sidebar shows "Semantic Releases" entry, active state highlights correctly

     1→package api

    10→// SemanticReleasesStore defines the persistence operations for semantic releases.
    11→type SemanticReleasesStore interface {
    12→	ListSemanticReleases(ctx context.Context, projectID string, page, perPage int) ([]models.SemanticRelease, int, error)
    13→	GetSemanticRelease(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.SemanticRelease, error)
    14→	GetSemanticReleaseSources(ctx context.Context, id string) ([]models.Release, error)
    15→	DeleteSemanticRelease(ctx context.Context, id string) error
    16→}
    17→
    18→// SemanticReleasesHandler implements HTTP handlers for the /semantic-releases resource.
    19→type SemanticReleasesHandler struct {
    20→	store SemanticReleasesStore
    21→}
    22→
    23→// NewSemanticReleasesHandler returns a new SemanticReleasesHandler.
    24→func NewSemanticReleasesHandler(store SemanticReleasesStore) *SemanticReleasesHandler {
    25→	return &SemanticReleasesHandler{store: store}
    26→}
    27→
    28→// List handles GET /projects/{projectId}/semantic-releases — returns a paginated list of semantic releases for a project.
    29→func (h *SemanticReleasesHandler) List(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    30→	projectID := r.PathValue("projectId")
    31→	if projectID == "" {
    32→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Project ID is required")
    33→		return
    34→	}
    35→	page, perPage := ParsePagination(r)
    36→	releases, total, err := h.store.ListSemanticReleases(r.Context(), projectID, page, perPage)
    37→	if err != nil {
    38→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal_error", "Failed to list semantic releases")
    39→		return
    40→	}
    41→	if releases == nil {
    42→		releases = []models.SemanticRelease{}
    43→	}
    44→	RespondList(w, r, http.StatusOK, releases, page, perPage, total)
    45→}
    46→
    47→// Get handles GET /semantic-releases/{id} — returns a single semantic release.
    48→func (h *SemanticReleasesHandler) Get(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    49→	id := r.PathValue("id")
    50→	if id == "" {
    51→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Semantic release ID is required")
    52→		return
    53→	}
    54→	sr, err := h.store.GetSemanticRelease(r.Context(), id)
    55→	if err != nil {
    56→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusNotFound, "not_found", "Semantic release not found")
    57→		return
    58→	}
    59→	RespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusOK, sr)
    60→}
    61→
    62→// Delete handles DELETE /semantic-releases/{id} — deletes a single semantic release.
    63→func (h *SemanticReleasesHandler) Delete(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    64→	id := r.PathValue("id")
    65→	if id == "" {
    66→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Semantic release ID is required")
    67→		return
    68→	}
    69→	if err := h.store.DeleteSemanticRelease(r.Context(), id); err != nil {
    70→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusNotFound, "not_found", "Semantic release not found")
    71→		return
    72→	}
    73→	w.WriteHeader(http.StatusNoContent)
    74→}
    75→

     1→package api
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"

     9→	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"
    10→	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"
    11→	"github.com/riverqueue/river"
    12→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models"

    15→
    16→// PgStore implements all store interfaces using a PostgreSQL connection pool.
    17→type PgStore struct {
    18→	pool  *pgxpool.Pool
    19→	river *river.Client[pgx.Tx]
    20→}
    21→
    22→// NewPgStore returns a new PgStore backed by the given connection pool and River client.
    23→// The river client may be nil if agent triggering is not needed.
    24→func NewPgStore(pool *pgxpool.Pool, riverClient *river.Client[pgx.Tx]) *PgStore {
    25→	return &PgStore{pool: pool, river: riverClient}
    26→}
    27→
    28→// --- ProjectsStore ---
    29→
    30→func (s *PgStore) ListProjects(ctx context.Context, page, perPage int) ([]models.Project, int, error) {
    31→	var total int
    32→	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM projects`).Scan(&total)
    33→	if err != nil {
    34→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("count projects: %w", err)
    35→	}
    36→	offset := (page - 1) * perPage
    37→	rows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx,
    38→		`SELECT id, name, COALESCE(description,''), COALESCE(agent_prompt,''),
    39→		        COALESCE(agent_rules,'{}'), created_at, updated_at
    40→		 FROM projects ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT $1 OFFSET $2`, perPage, offset)
    41→	if err != nil {
    42→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("list projects: %w", err)
    43→	}
    44→	defer rows.Close()
    45→	var projects []models.Project
    46→	for rows.Next() {
    47→		var p models.Project
    48→		if err := rows.Scan(&p.ID, &p.Name, &p.Description, &p.AgentPrompt, &p.AgentRules, &p.CreatedAt, &p.UpdatedAt); err != nil {
    49→			return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("scan project: %w", err)
    50→		}
    51→		projects = append(projects, p)
    52→	}
    53→	return projects, total, nil
    54→}
    55→
    56→func (s *PgStore) CreateProject(ctx context.Context, p *models.Project) error {
    57→	return s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,
    58→		`INSERT INTO projects (name, description, agent_prompt, agent_rules)
    59→		 VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4)
    60→		 RETURNING id, created_at, updated_at`,
    61→		p.Name, p.Description, p.AgentPrompt, p.AgentRules,
    62→	).Scan(&p.ID, &p.CreatedAt, &p.UpdatedAt)
    63→}
    64→
    65→func (s *PgStore) GetProject(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.Project, error) {
    66→	var p models.Project
    67→	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,
    68→		`SELECT id, name, COALESCE(description,''), COALESCE(agent_prompt,''),
    69→		        COALESCE(agent_rules,'{}'), created_at, updated_at
    70→		 FROM projects WHERE id = $1`, id,
    71→	).Scan(&p.ID, &p.Name, &p.Description, &p.AgentPrompt, &p.AgentRules, &p.CreatedAt, &p.UpdatedAt)
    72→	if err != nil {
    73→		return nil, err
    74→	}
    75→	return &p, nil
    76→}
    77→
    78→func (s *PgStore) UpdateProject(ctx context.Context, id string, p *models.Project) error {
    79→	return s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,
    80→		`UPDATE projects SET name=$1, description=$2, agent_prompt=$3, agent_rules=$4, updated_at=NOW()
    81→		 WHERE id=$5 RETURNING updated_at`,
    82→		p.Name, p.Description, p.AgentPrompt, p.AgentRules, id,
    83→	).Scan(&p.UpdatedAt)
    84→}
    85→
    86→func (s *PgStore) DeleteProject(ctx context.Context, id string) error {
    87→	tag, err := s.pool.Exec(ctx, `DELETE FROM projects WHERE id = $1`, id)
    88→	if err != nil {
    89→		return err
    90→	}
    91→	if tag.RowsAffected() == 0 {
    92→		return fmt.Errorf("not found")
    93→	}
    94→	return nil
    95→}
    96→
    97→// --- KeyStore ---
    98→
    99→func (s *PgStore) ValidateKey(ctx context.Context, rawKey string) (bool, error) {
   100→	hash := sha256.Sum256([]byte(rawKey))
   101→	keyHash := hex.EncodeToString(hash[:])
   102→	var exists bool
   103→	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,
   104→		`SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM api_keys WHERE key_hash = $1)`, keyHash,
   105→	).Scan(&exists)
   106→	return exists, err
   107→}
   108→
   109→func (s *PgStore) TouchKeyUsage(ctx context.Context, rawKey string) {
   110→	hash := sha256.Sum256([]byte(rawKey))
   111→	keyHash := hex.EncodeToString(hash[:])
   112→	_, _ = s.pool.Exec(ctx, `UPDATE api_keys SET last_used_at = NOW() WHERE key_hash = $1`, keyHash)
   113→}
   114→
   115→// --- SourcesStore ---
   116→
   117→func (s *PgStore) ListSourcesByProject(ctx context.Context, projectID string, page, perPage int) ([]models.Source, int, error) {
   118→	var total int
   119→	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sources WHERE project_id = $1`, projectID).Scan(&total)
   120→	if err != nil {
   121→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("count sources: %w", err)
   122→	}
   123→	offset := (page - 1) * perPage
   124→	rows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx,
   125→		`SELECT id, project_id, provider, repository, poll_interval_seconds, enabled,
   126→		        COALESCE(config,'{}'), last_polled_at, last_error, created_at, updated_at
   127→		 FROM sources WHERE project_id = $1 ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT $2 OFFSET $3`, projectID, perPage, offset)
   128→	if err != nil {
   129→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("list sources: %w", err)
   130→	}
   131→	defer rows.Close()
   132→	var sources []models.Source
   133→	for rows.Next() {
   134→		var src models.Source
   135→		if err := rows.Scan(&src.ID, &src.ProjectID, &src.Provider, &src.Repository,
   136→			&src.PollIntervalSeconds, &src.Enabled, &src.Config,
   137→			&src.LastPolledAt, &src.LastError, &src.CreatedAt, &src.UpdatedAt); err != nil {
   138→			return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("scan source: %w", err)
   139→		}
   140→		sources = append(sources, src)
   141→	}
   142→	return sources, total, nil
   143→}
   144→
   145→func (s *PgStore) CreateSource(ctx context.Context, src *models.Source) error {
   146→	return s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,
   147→		`INSERT INTO sources (project_id, provider, repository, poll_interval_seconds, enabled, config)
   148→		 VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6)
   149→		 RETURNING id, enabled, created_at, updated_at`,
   150→		src.ProjectID, src.Provider, src.Repository, src.PollIntervalSeconds, src.Enabled, src.Config,
   151→	).Scan(&src.ID, &src.Enabled, &src.CreatedAt, &src.UpdatedAt)

   153→
   154→func (s *PgStore) GetSource(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.Source, error) {
   155→	var src models.Source
   156→	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,
   157→		`SELECT id, project_id, provider, repository, poll_interval_seconds, enabled,

   161→		&src.PollIntervalSeconds, &src.Enabled, &src.Config,

   163→	if err != nil {

   168→
   169→func (s *PgStore) UpdateSource(ctx context.Context, id string, src *models.Source) error {
   170→	return s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,
   171→		`UPDATE sources SET provider=$1, repository=$2, poll_interval_seconds=$3, enabled=$4,
   172→		        config=$5, updated_at=NOW()
   173→		 WHERE id=$6 RETURNING updated_at`,
   174→		src.Provider, src.Repository, src.PollIntervalSeconds, src.Enabled, src.Config, id,
   175→	).Scan(&src.UpdatedAt)

   177→
   178→func (s *PgStore) DeleteSource(ctx context.Context, id string) error {

   559→// --- AgentStore ---
   560→
   561→func (s *PgStore) TriggerAgentRun(ctx context.Context, projectID, trigger string) (*models.AgentRun, error) {
   562→	var run models.AgentRun
   563→	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,
   564→		`INSERT INTO agent_runs (project_id, trigger, status)
   565→		 VALUES ($1, $2, 'pending')
   566→		 RETURNING id, project_id, trigger, status, created_at`,
   567→		projectID, trigger,
   568→	).Scan(&run.ID, &run.ProjectID, &run.Trigger, &run.Status, &run.CreatedAt)
   569→	if err != nil {
   570→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("insert agent run: %w", err)
   571→	}
   572→
   573→	// Enqueue a River job to process the agent run, if river client is available.
   574→	if s.river != nil {
   575→		_, err = s.river.Insert(ctx, queue.AgentJobArgs{
   576→			AgentRunID: run.ID,
   577→			ProjectID:  projectID,
   578→		}, nil)
   579→		if err != nil {
   580→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("enqueue agent job: %w", err)
   581→		}
   582→	}
   583→
   584→	return &run, nil
   585→}
   586→
   587→func (s *PgStore) ListAgentRuns(ctx context.Context, projectID string, page, perPage int) ([]models.AgentRun, int, error) {
   588→	var total int
   589→	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM agent_runs WHERE project_id = $1`, projectID).Scan(&total)
   590→	if err != nil {
   591→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("count agent runs: %w", err)
   592→	}
   593→	offset := (page - 1) * perPage
   594→	rows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx,
   595→		`SELECT id, project_id, semantic_release_id, trigger, status,
   596→		        COALESCE(prompt_used,''), COALESCE(error,''),
   597→		        started_at, completed_at, created_at
   598→		 FROM agent_runs WHERE project_id = $1 ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT $2 OFFSET $3`, projectID, perPage, offset)
   599→	if err != nil {
   600→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("list agent runs: %w", err)
   601→	}
   602→	defer rows.Close()
   603→	var runs []models.AgentRun
   604→	for rows.Next() {
   605→		var run models.AgentRun
   606→		if err := rows.Scan(&run.ID, &run.ProjectID, &run.SemanticReleaseID, &run.Trigger, &run.Status,
   607→			&run.PromptUsed, &run.Error, &run.StartedAt, &run.CompletedAt, &run.CreatedAt); err != nil {
   608→			return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("scan agent run: %w", err)
   609→		}
   610→		runs = append(runs, run)
   611→	}
   612→	return runs, total, nil
   613→}

   632→// ListSourceSubscriptions returns all source-level subscriptions for a given source.

   634→	rows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx,
   635→		`SELECT id, channel_id, type, source_id, project_id, COALESCE(version_filter,''), created_at
   636→		 FROM subscriptions
   637→		 WHERE type = 'source' AND source_id = $1`, sourceID)
   638→	if err != nil {
   639→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("list source subscriptions: %w", err)
   640→	}
   641→	defer rows.Close()
   642→	var subs []models.Subscription
   643→	for rows.Next() {
   644→		var sub models.Subscription
   645→		if err := rows.Scan(&sub.ID, &sub.ChannelID, &sub.Type, &sub.SourceID,
   646→			&sub.ProjectID, &sub.VersionFilter, &sub.CreatedAt); err != nil {
   647→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("scan subscription: %w", err)

   653→
   654→// GetPreviousRelease returns the most recent release for a source that is not
   655→// the given version, ordered by created_at descending. Returns nil (no error)
   656→// if no previous release exists.
   657→func (s *PgStore) GetPreviousRelease(ctx context.Context, sourceID string, beforeVersion string) (*models.Release, error) {
   658→	var rel models.Release
   659→	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,
   660→		`SELECT id, source_id, version, COALESCE(raw_data,'{}'), released_at, created_at
   661→		 FROM releases
   662→		 WHERE source_id = $1 AND version != $2
   663→		 ORDER BY COALESCE(released_at, created_at) DESC
   664→		 LIMIT 1`, sourceID, beforeVersion,
   665→	).Scan(&rel.ID, &rel.SourceID, &rel.Version, &rel.RawData, &rel.ReleasedAt, &rel.CreatedAt)
   666→	if err != nil {
   667→		if err.Error() == "no rows in result set" {
   668→			return nil, nil
   669→		}
   670→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("get previous release: %w", err)
   671→	}
   672→	return &rel, nil
   673→}
   674→
   675→// EnqueueAgentRun creates an agent_run row and enqueues a River AgentJobArgs.
   676→// This follows the transactional outbox pattern for zero-loss guarantee.
   677→func (s *PgStore) EnqueueAgentRun(ctx context.Context, projectID, trigger string) error {
   678→	tx, err := s.pool.Begin(ctx)
   679→	if err != nil {
   680→		return fmt.Errorf("begin tx: %w", err)
   681→	}
   682→	defer tx.Rollback(ctx)
   683→
   684→	var runID string
   685→	err = tx.QueryRow(ctx,
   686→		`INSERT INTO agent_runs (project_id, trigger, status)
   687→		 VALUES ($1, $2, 'pending')
   688→		 RETURNING id`, projectID, trigger,
   689→	).Scan(&runID)
   690→	if err != nil {
   691→		return fmt.Errorf("insert agent run: %w", err)
   692→	}
   693→
   694→	if s.river != nil {
   695→		_, err = s.river.InsertTx(ctx, tx, queue.AgentJobArgs{
   696→			AgentRunID: runID,
   697→			ProjectID:  projectID,
   698→		}, nil)
   699→		if err != nil {
   700→			return fmt.Errorf("enqueue agent job: %w", err)
   701→		}
   702→	}
   703→
   704→	return tx.Commit(ctx)
   705→}
   706→
   707→// --- OrchestratorStore (agent layer) ---
   708→
   709→// UpdateAgentRunStatus sets the status of an agent run and updates the
   710→// started_at timestamp when transitioning to "running".
   711→func (s *PgStore) UpdateAgentRunStatus(ctx context.Context, id, status string) error {
   712→	var query string
   713→	switch status {
   714→	case "running":
   715→		query = `UPDATE agent_runs SET status = $1, started_at = NOW() WHERE id = $2`
   716→	case "completed":
   717→		query = `UPDATE agent_runs SET status = $1, completed_at = NOW() WHERE id = $2`
   718→	default:
   719→		query = `UPDATE agent_runs SET status = $1 WHERE id = $2`
   720→	}
   721→	tag, err := s.pool.Exec(ctx, query, status, id)
   722→	if err != nil {
   723→		return fmt.Errorf("update agent run status: %w", err)
   724→	}
   725→	if tag.RowsAffected() == 0 {
   726→		return fmt.Errorf("agent run not found: %s", id)
   727→	}
   728→	return nil
   729→}
   730→
   731→// CreateSemanticRelease inserts a semantic release and its source release links
   732→// in a single transaction. The sr.ID field is populated on success.
   733→func (s *PgStore) CreateSemanticRelease(ctx context.Context, sr *models.SemanticRelease, releaseIDs []string) error {
   734→	tx, err := s.pool.Begin(ctx)
   735→	if err != nil {
   736→		return fmt.Errorf("begin tx: %w", err)
   737→	}
   738→	defer tx.Rollback(ctx)
   739→
   740→	err = tx.QueryRow(ctx,
   741→		`INSERT INTO semantic_releases (project_id, version, report, status, completed_at)
   742→		 VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)
   743→		 RETURNING id, created_at`,
   744→		sr.ProjectID, sr.Version, sr.Report, sr.Status, sr.CompletedAt,
   745→	).Scan(&sr.ID, &sr.CreatedAt)
   746→	if err != nil {
   747→		return fmt.Errorf("insert semantic release: %w", err)
   748→	}
   749→
   750→	for _, relID := range releaseIDs {
   751→		_, err = tx.Exec(ctx,
   752→			`INSERT INTO semantic_release_sources (semantic_release_id, release_id)
   753→			 VALUES ($1, $2) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`,
   754→			sr.ID, relID,
   755→		)
   756→		if err != nil {
   757→			return fmt.Errorf("insert semantic release source: %w", err)
   758→		}
   759→	}
   760→
   761→	return tx.Commit(ctx)
   762→}
   763→
   764→// UpdateAgentRunResult sets the semantic_release_id for a completed agent run.
   765→func (s *PgStore) UpdateAgentRunResult(ctx context.Context, id string, semanticReleaseID string) error {
   766→	tag, err := s.pool.Exec(ctx,
   767→		`UPDATE agent_runs SET semantic_release_id = $1 WHERE id = $2`,
   768→		semanticReleaseID, id,
   769→	)
   770→	if err != nil {
   771→		return fmt.Errorf("update agent run result: %w", err)
   772→	}
   773→	if tag.RowsAffected() == 0 {
   774→		return fmt.Errorf("agent run not found: %s", id)
   775→	}
   776→	return nil
   777→}
   778→
   779→// ListProjectSubscriptions returns all project-level subscriptions for a given project.
   780→func (s *PgStore) ListProjectSubscriptions(ctx context.Context, projectID string) ([]models.Subscription, error) {
   781→	rows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx,
   782→		`SELECT id, channel_id, type, source_id, project_id, COALESCE(version_filter,''), created_at
   783→		 FROM subscriptions
   784→		 WHERE type = 'project' AND project_id = $1`, projectID)
   785→	if err != nil {
   786→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("list project subscriptions: %w", err)
   787→	}
   788→	defer rows.Close()
   789→	var subs []models.Subscription
   790→	for rows.Next() {
   791→		var sub models.Subscription
   792→		if err := rows.Scan(&sub.ID, &sub.ChannelID, &sub.Type, &sub.SourceID,
   793→			&sub.ProjectID, &sub.VersionFilter, &sub.CreatedAt); err != nil {
   794→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("scan subscription: %w", err)
   795→		}
   796→		subs = append(subs, sub)
   797→	}
   798→	return subs, nil
   799→}
   800→
   801→// --- HealthChecker ---
   802→
   803→func (s *PgStore) Ping(ctx context.Context) error {
   804→	return s.pool.Ping(ctx)
   805→}
   806→
   807→func (s *PgStore) PingDB(ctx context.Context) error {
   808→	return s.pool.Ping(ctx)
   809→}
   810→
   811→func (s *PgStore) GetStats(ctx context.Context) (*DashboardStats, error) {
   812→	var stats DashboardStats
   813→	if err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM releases`).Scan(&stats.TotalReleases); err != nil {
   814→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("count releases: %w", err)
   815→	}
   816→	if err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sources WHERE enabled = true`).Scan(&stats.ActiveSources); err != nil {
   817→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("count active sources: %w", err)
   818→	}
   819→	if err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM projects`).Scan(&stats.TotalProjects); err != nil {
   820→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("count projects: %w", err)
   821→	}
   822→	if err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM agent_runs WHERE status = 'pending'`).Scan(&stats.PendingAgentRuns); err != nil {
   823→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("count pending agent runs: %w", err)
   824→	}
   825→	return &stats, nil
   826→}
   827→

     1→package api

     3→import (
     4→	"net/http"

     6→	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"
     7→)
     8→
     9→// Dependencies holds all external dependencies required for registering API routes.
    10→type Dependencies struct {
    11→	DB                    *pgxpool.Pool
    12→	ProjectsStore         ProjectsStore
    13→	ReleasesStore         ReleasesStore
    14→	SubscriptionsStore    SubscriptionsStore
    15→	SourcesStore          SourcesStore
    16→	ChannelsStore         ChannelsStore
    17→	ContextSourcesStore   ContextSourcesStore
    18→	SemanticReleasesStore SemanticReleasesStore
    19→	AgentStore            AgentStore
    20→	KeyStore              KeyStore
    21→	HealthChecker         HealthChecker
    22→	Broadcaster           *Broadcaster
    23→	NoAuth                bool
    24→}
    25→
    26→// RegisterRoutes registers all API v1 routes on the given ServeMux.
    27→func RegisterRoutes(mux *http.ServeMux, deps Dependencies) {
    28→	// Public chain: no auth required.
    29→	publicChain := Chain(RequestID, Logger, Recovery)
    30→
    31→	// Authenticated chain: includes auth unless NoAuth is set.
    32→	chain := publicChain
    33→	if !deps.NoAuth {
    34→		chain = Chain(RequestID, Logger, Recovery, RateLimit(10, 20), Auth(deps.KeyStore))
    35→	}
    36→
    37→	// Projects (CRUD)
    38→	projects := NewProjectsHandler(deps.ProjectsStore)
    39→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/projects", chain(http.HandlerFunc(projects.List)))
    40→	mux.Handle("POST /api/v1/projects", chain(http.HandlerFunc(projects.Create)))
    41→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/projects/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(projects.Get)))
    42→	mux.Handle("PUT /api/v1/projects/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(projects.Update)))
    43→	mux.Handle("DELETE /api/v1/projects/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(projects.Delete)))
    44→
    45→	// Sources (nested under projects)
    46→	sources := NewSourcesHandler(deps.SourcesStore)
    47→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/projects/{projectId}/sources", chain(http.HandlerFunc(sources.List)))
    48→	mux.Handle("POST /api/v1/projects/{projectId}/sources", chain(http.HandlerFunc(sources.Create)))
    49→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/sources/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(sources.Get)))
    50→	mux.Handle("PUT /api/v1/sources/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(sources.Update)))
    51→	mux.Handle("DELETE /api/v1/sources/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(sources.Delete)))
    52→
    53→	// Context Sources (nested under projects)
    54→	contextSources := NewContextSourcesHandler(deps.ContextSourcesStore)
    55→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/projects/{projectId}/context-sources", chain(http.HandlerFunc(contextSources.List)))
    56→	mux.Handle("POST /api/v1/projects/{projectId}/context-sources", chain(http.HandlerFunc(contextSources.Create)))
    57→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/context-sources/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(contextSources.Get)))
    58→	mux.Handle("PUT /api/v1/context-sources/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(contextSources.Update)))
    59→	mux.Handle("DELETE /api/v1/context-sources/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(contextSources.Delete)))
    60→
    61→	// Releases (read-only, nested under sources and projects)
    62→	releases := NewReleasesHandler(deps.ReleasesStore)
    63→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/releases", chain(http.HandlerFunc(releases.List)))
    64→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/sources/{id}/releases", chain(http.HandlerFunc(releases.ListBySource)))
    65→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/projects/{projectId}/releases", chain(http.HandlerFunc(releases.ListByProject)))
    66→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/releases/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(releases.Get)))
    67→
    68→	// Semantic Releases (nested under projects)
    69→	semanticReleases := NewSemanticReleasesHandler(deps.SemanticReleasesStore)
    70→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/projects/{projectId}/semantic-releases", chain(http.HandlerFunc(semanticReleases.List)))
    71→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/semantic-releases/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(semanticReleases.Get)))
    72→	mux.Handle("DELETE /api/v1/semantic-releases/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(semanticReleases.Delete)))
    73→
    74→	// Subscriptions (CRUD)
    75→	subscriptions := NewSubscriptionsHandler(deps.SubscriptionsStore)
    76→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/subscriptions", chain(http.HandlerFunc(subscriptions.List)))
    77→	mux.Handle("POST /api/v1/subscriptions", chain(http.HandlerFunc(subscriptions.Create)))
    78→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/subscriptions/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(subscriptions.Get)))
    79→	mux.Handle("PUT /api/v1/subscriptions/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(subscriptions.Update)))
    80→	mux.Handle("DELETE /api/v1/subscriptions/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(subscriptions.Delete)))
    81→
    82→	// Channels (CRUD)
    83→	channels := NewChannelsHandler(deps.ChannelsStore)
    84→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/channels", chain(http.HandlerFunc(channels.List)))
    85→	mux.Handle("POST /api/v1/channels", chain(http.HandlerFunc(channels.Create)))
    86→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/channels/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(channels.Get)))
    87→	mux.Handle("PUT /api/v1/channels/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(channels.Update)))
    88→	mux.Handle("DELETE /api/v1/channels/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(channels.Delete)))
    89→
    90→	// Agent
    91→	agent := NewAgentHandler(deps.AgentStore)
    92→	mux.Handle("POST /api/v1/projects/{projectId}/agent/run", chain(http.HandlerFunc(agent.TriggerRun)))
    93→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/projects/{projectId}/agent/runs", chain(http.HandlerFunc(agent.ListRuns)))
    94→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/agent-runs/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(agent.GetRun)))
    95→
    96→	// Providers (metadata — static, no store needed)
    97→	providers := NewProvidersHandler()
    98→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/providers", chain(http.HandlerFunc(providers.List)))
    99→
   100→	// SSE events
   101→	events := NewEventsHandler(deps.Broadcaster)
   102→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/events", chain(http.HandlerFunc(events.Stream)))
   103→
   104→	// Health (public — no auth middleware)
   105→	health := NewHealthHandler(deps.HealthChecker)
   106→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/health", publicChain(http.HandlerFunc(health.Check)))
   107→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/stats", chain(http.HandlerFunc(health.Stats)))
   108→}

    25→func (m *mockSemanticReleasesStore) ListSemanticReleases(_ context.Context, projectID string, page, perPage int) ([]models.SemanticRelease, int, error) {
    26→	if m.listErr != nil {
    27→		return nil, 0, m.listErr

    32→func (m *mockSemanticReleasesStore) GetSemanticRelease(_ context.Context, id string) (*models.SemanticRelease, error) {
    33→	if m.getErr != nil {
    34→		return nil, m.getErr
    35→	}
    36→	for i := range m.releases {
    37→		if m.releases[i].ID == id {
    38→			return &m.releases[i], nil
    39→		}
    40→	}
    41→	return nil, fmt.Errorf("not found")

    43→
    44→func (m *mockSemanticReleasesStore) GetSemanticReleaseSources(_ context.Context, id string) ([]models.Release, error) {

    48→	return m.sources, nil

    50→
    51→func (m *mockSemanticReleasesStore) DeleteSemanticRelease(_ context.Context, id string) error {
    52→	if m.deleteErr != nil {
    53→		return m.deleteErr
    54→	}
    55→	for i := range m.releases {
    56→		if m.releases[i].ID == id {
    57→			m.releases = append(m.releases[:i], m.releases[i+1:]...)
    58→			return nil
    59→		}
    60→	}
    61→	return fmt.Errorf("not found")

     1→// web/lib/api/client.ts
     2→import type {
     3→  ApiResponse,
     4→  Project,
     5→  ProjectInput,
     6→  Release,
     7→  Source,
     8→  SourceInput,
     9→  Subscription,
    10→  SubscriptionInput,
    11→  NotificationChannel,
    12→  ChannelInput,
    13→  ContextSource,
    14→  ContextSourceInput,
    15→  SemanticRelease,
    16→  AgentRun,
    17→  HealthStatus,
    18→  Stats,
    19→} from "./types";
    20→
    21→const BASE = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL || "/api/v1";
    22→
    23→async function request<T>(path: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {
    24→  const res = await fetch(`${BASE}${path}`, {
    25→    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...init?.headers },
    26→    ...init,
    27→  });
    28→  if (!res.ok) {
    29→    const body = await res.json().catch(() => null);
    30→    throw new Error(body?.error?.message ?? `Request failed: ${res.status}`);
    31→  }
    32→  if (res.status === 204) return null as T;
    33→  return res.json();
    34→}
    35→
    36→// --- Projects ---
    37→
    38→export const projects = {
    39→  list: (page = 1, perPage = 25) =>
    40→    request<ApiResponse<Project[]>>(`/projects?page=${page}&per_page=${perPage}`),
    41→  get: (id: string) =>
    42→    request<ApiResponse<Project>>(`/projects/${id}`),
    43→  create: (input: ProjectInput) =>
    44→    request<ApiResponse<Project>>("/projects", {
    45→      method: "POST",
    46→      body: JSON.stringify(input),
    47→    }),
    48→  update: (id: string, input: ProjectInput) =>
    49→    request<ApiResponse<Project>>(`/projects/${id}`, {
    50→      method: "PUT",
    51→      body: JSON.stringify(input),
    52→    }),
    53→  delete: (id: string) =>
    54→    request<ApiResponse<null>>(`/projects/${id}`, { method: "DELETE" }),
    55→};
    56→
    57→// --- Sources (nested under projects) ---
    58→
    59→export const sources = {
    60→  listByProject: (projectId: string, page = 1) =>
    61→    request<ApiResponse<Source[]>>(`/projects/${projectId}/sources?page=${page}`),
    62→  create: (projectId: string, input: SourceInput) =>
    63→    request<ApiResponse<Source>>(`/projects/${projectId}/sources`, {
    64→      method: "POST",
    65→      body: JSON.stringify(input),
    66→    }),
    67→  get: (id: string) =>
    68→    request<ApiResponse<Source>>(`/sources/${id}`),
    69→  update: (id: string, input: SourceInput) =>
    70→    request<ApiResponse<Source>>(`/sources/${id}`, {
    71→      method: "PUT",
    72→      body: JSON.stringify(input),
    73→    }),
    74→  delete: (id: string) =>
    75→    request<ApiResponse<null>>(`/sources/${id}`, { method: "DELETE" }),
    76→};
    77→
    78→// --- Releases ---
    79→
    80→export const releases = {
    81→  list: (page = 1, perPage = 25) =>

    83→  listBySource: (sourceId: string, page = 1) =>

     1→"use client";
     2→
     3→import { useState } from "react";
     4→import useSWR from "swr";
     5→import Link from "next/link";
     6→import { semanticReleases as srApi } from "@/lib/api/client";

    10→import { timeAgo } from "@/lib/format";
    11→
    12→/* ---------- Urgency chip ---------- */
    13→
    14→const URGENCY_COLORS: Record<string, { bg: string; text: string }> = {
    15→  critical: { bg: "#dc2626", text: "#ffffff" },
    16→  high: { bg: "#f97316", text: "#ffffff" },
    17→};
    18→
    19→function UrgencyChip({ urgency }: { urgency: string }) {
    20→  const u = urgency.toLowerCase();
    21→  const style = URGENCY_COLORS[u];
    22→  if (!style) return null;
    23→  return (
    24→    <span
    25→      className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-medium leading-none"
    26→      style={{ backgroundColor: style.bg, color: style.text }}
    27→    >
    28→      {urgency}
    29→    </span>
    30→  );
    31→}
    32→
    33→/* ---------- Helpers ---------- */
    34→
    35→function truncate(str: string, max: number): string {
    36→  return str.length > max ? str.slice(0, max) + "\u2026" : str;
    37→}
    38→

   151→                style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}
   152→                title="Delete semantic release"

   182→        onConfirm={async () => {

    72→

    77→          style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
    78→        >
    79→          {project.name}
    80→        </p>
    81→      )}
    82→
    83→      {/* 3. Version heading */}
    84→      <h1
    85→        className="text-[42px] font-bold tracking-tight text-[#111113] leading-[1.1]"
    86→        style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}

    89→      </h1>

   180→            >

   182→            </blockquote>

   186→
   187→      {/* 11. Source Releases section */}
   188→      {releasesList.length > 0 && (

   191→          <div

   193→            style={{ border: "1px solid #e8e8e5" }}
   194→          >
   195→            <table className="w-full text-left">
   196→              <thead>
   197→                <tr style={{ backgroundColor: "#fafaf9", borderBottom: "1px solid #e8e8e5" }}>
   198→                  <th
   199→                    className="px-4 py-2.5 text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-[#9ca3af]"
   200→                    style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   201→                  >
   202→                    Provider

   204→                  <th
   205→                    className="px-4 py-2.5 text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-[#9ca3af]"
   206→                    style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   207→                  >
   208→                    Repository
   209→                  </th>
   210→                  <th
   211→                    className="px-4 py-2.5 text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-[#9ca3af]"
   212→                    style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   213→                  >
   214→                    Version
   215→                  </th>
   216→                  <th
   217→                    className="px-4 py-2.5 text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-[#9ca3af]"
   218→                    style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   219→                  >
   220→                    Date
   221→                  </th>
   222→                </tr>
   223→              </thead>
   224→              <tbody>
   225→                {releasesList.map((rel) => {
   226→                  const source = sourcesById[rel.source_id];

   228→                    <tr
   229→                      key={rel.id}
   230→                      style={{ borderBottom: "1px solid #e8e8e5" }}
   231→                      className="last:border-b-0"
   232→                    >
   233→                      <td className="px-4 py-3">
   234→                        {source ? (
   235→                          <ProviderBadge provider={source.provider} />
   236→                        ) : (
   237→                          <span className="text-[12px] text-[#9ca3af]">{"\u2014"}</span>
   238→                        )}
   239→                      </td>
   240→                      <td className="px-4 py-3">
   241→                        <span
   242→                          className="text-[13px] text-[#374151]"
   243→                          style={{ fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace" }}
   244→                        >
   245→                          {source?.repository ?? "\u2014"}
   246→                        </span>
   247→                      </td>
   248→                      <td className="px-4 py-3">
   249→                        <VersionChip version={rel.version} />
   250→                      </td>
   251→                      <td className="px-4 py-3">
   252→                        <span
   253→                          className="text-[13px] text-[#6b7280]"
   254→                          style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   255→                        >
   256→                          {timeAgo(rel.released_at ?? rel.created_at)}
   257→                        </span>
   258→                      </td>
   259→                    </tr>
   260→                  );
   261→                })}
   262→              </tbody>
   263→            </table>
   264→          </div>
   265→        </section>
   266→      )}
   267→    </div>
   268→  );
   269→}

     6→import {
     7→  LayoutDashboard,

    10→  Bell,

    15→import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
    16→
    17→const navItems = [
    18→  { href: "/", label: "Dashboard", icon: LayoutDashboard },
    19→  { href: "/projects", label: "Projects", icon: FolderKanban },
    20→  { href: "/releases", label: "Releases", icon: Package },

    22→  { href: "/subscriptions", label: "Subscriptions", icon: Bell },
    23→];
    24→
    25→export function Sidebar() {
    26→  const pathname = usePathname();
    27→  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState(false);
    28→
    29→  return (
    30→    <aside
    31→      className={cn(
    32→        "flex shrink-0 flex-col transition-[width] duration-200",
    33→        expanded ? "w-[200px]" : "w-[52px]"
    34→      )}
    35→      style={{ backgroundColor: "#16181c" }}
    36→    >
    37→      {/* Logo + toggle */}
    38→      <div className="flex h-12 items-center px-3">
    39→        {expanded ? (
    40→          <>
    41→            <img src="/logo.svg" alt="" className="h-7 w-7 shrink-0" />
    42→            <Link
    43→              href="/"
    44→              className="ml-2 text-[16px] italic text-white"
    45→              style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}

    47→              Changelogue
    48→            </Link>
    49→            <button
    50→              onClick={() => setExpanded(false)}
    51→              className="ml-auto text-[#9ca3af] transition-colors hover:text-white"
    52→              title="Collapse sidebar"
    53→            >
    54→              <PanelLeftClose className="h-4 w-4" />
    55→            </button>
    56→          </>
    57→        ) : (
    58→          <button
    59→            onClick={() => setExpanded(true)}
    60→            className="mx-auto text-[#9ca3af] transition-colors hover:text-white"
    61→            title="Expand sidebar"
    62→          >
    63→            <PanelLeftOpen className="h-4 w-4" />
    64→          </button>
    65→        )}
    66→      </div>
    67→
    68→      {/* Nav */}
    69→      <nav className="flex-1 pt-2">
    70→        {navItems.map((item) => {
    71→          const isActive =
    72→            item.href === "/"
    73→              ? pathname === "/"
    74→              : pathname.startsWith(item.href);
    75→          return (
    76→            <Link
    77→              key={item.href}
    78→              href={item.href}
    79→              title={expanded ? undefined : item.label}
    80→              className={cn(
    81→                "flex items-center gap-3 py-2 text-[13px] transition-colors duration-150",
    82→                expanded ? "pl-4 pr-3" : "justify-center px-0",

    85→                  : "text-[#9ca3af] hover:text-white"
    86→              )}
    87→              style={
    88→                isActive
    89→                  ? {
    90→                      borderLeft: "3px solid #e8601a",
    91→                      backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.06)",
    92→                      paddingLeft: expanded ? "13px" : undefined,
    93→                    }
    94→                  : { borderLeft: "3px solid transparent" }
    95→              }
    96→            >
    97→              <item.icon className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
    98→              {expanded && (
    99→                <span style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}>
   100→                  {item.label}
   101→                </span>
   102→              )}
   103→            </Link>
   104→          );
   105→        })}
   106→      </nav>
   107→    </aside>
   108→  );
   109→}

     1→"use client";

     3→import { useState } from "react";

     5→import Link from "next/link";
     6→import {
     7→  projects as projectsApi,
     8→  releases as releasesApi,
     9→  sources as sourcesApi,

    18→
    19→/* ---------- Inline Add Source Form ---------- */
    20→
    21→function InlineSourceForm({
    22→  projectId,
    23→  onDone,
    24→}: {
    25→  projectId: string;
    26→  onDone: () => void;
    27→}) {
    28→  const [provider, setProvider] = useState("github");
    29→  const [repository, setRepository] = useState("");
    30→  const [pollInterval, setPollInterval] = useState("300");
    31→  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
    32→  const [error, setError] = useState("");
    33→
    34→  const handleAdd = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    35→    e.preventDefault();
    36→    if (!repository.trim()) return;
    37→    setSaving(true);
    38→    setError("");
    39→    try {
    40→      await sourcesApi.create(projectId, {
    41→        provider,
    42→        repository: repository.trim(),
    43→        poll_interval_seconds: Number(pollInterval) || 300,
    44→        enabled: true,
    45→      });
    46→      mutate(`project-${projectId}-card-sources`);
    47→      onDone();
    48→    } catch (err: unknown) {
    49→      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to add source");
    50→    } finally {
    51→      setSaving(false);
    52→    }
    53→  };
    54→
    55→  return (
    56→    <form onSubmit={handleAdd} className="mt-2 rounded-md border p-3 space-y-2" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#fafaf9" }}>
    57→      {error && <div className="text-[12px] text-red-600">{error}</div>}
    58→      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
    59→        <select
    60→          value={provider}
    61→          onChange={(e) => setProvider(e.target.value)}
    62→          className="rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[12px]"
    63→          style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
    64→        >
    65→          <option value="github">GitHub</option>
    66→          <option value="dockerhub">Docker Hub</option>
    67→        </select>
    68→        <input
    69→          type="text"
    70→          value={repository}
    71→          onChange={(e) => setRepository(e.target.value)}
    72→          placeholder="e.g. ethereum/go-ethereum"
    73→          className="flex-1 rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[12px]"
    74→          style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace" }}
    75→        />
    76→        <input
    77→          type="number"
    78→          min={60}
    79→          value={pollInterval}
    80→          onChange={(e) => setPollInterval(e.target.value)}
    81→          className="w-20 rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[12px]"
    82→          style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}
    83→          title="Poll interval (seconds)"
    84→        />
    85→      </div>
    86→      <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
    87→        <button
    88→          type="button"
    89→          onClick={onDone}
    90→          className="text-[12px] text-[#9ca3af] hover:text-[#6b7280]"
    91→        >
    92→          Cancel
    93→        </button>
    94→        <button
    95→          type="submit"
    96→          disabled={saving || !repository.trim()}
    97→          className="rounded-md px-3 py-1 text-[12px] font-medium text-white disabled:opacity-40"
    98→          style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a" }}
    99→        >
   100→          {saving ? "Adding..." : "Add"}
   101→        </button>
   102→      </div>
   103→    </form>
   104→  );
   105→}
   106→
   107→/* ---------- Sources Section ---------- */
   108→
   109→function SourcesSection({ projectId }: { projectId: string }) {

   114→  const sources = data?.data ?? [];
   115→
   116→  return (

   118→      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
   119→        <span
   120→          className="text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]"
   121→          style={{ color: "#9ca3af", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   122→        >
   123→          Sources
   124→        </span>
   125→        {!adding && (
   126→          <button
   127→            onClick={() => setAdding(true)}
   128→            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-medium transition-colors hover:opacity-80"
   129→            style={{ color: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   130→          >
   131→            <Plus className="h-3 w-3" />
   132→            Add Source
   133→          </button>
   134→        )}
   135→        {adding && (
   136→          <button
   137→            onClick={() => setAdding(false)}
   138→            className="text-[#9ca3af] hover:text-[#6b7280]"
   139→          >
   140→            <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   141→          </button>
   142→        )}
   143→      </div>
   144→
   145→      {sources.length > 0 ? (
   146→        <div className="space-y-1">
   147→          {sources.map((source) => {
   148→            const Icon = getProviderIcon(source.provider);
   149→            return (
   150→              <div
   151→                key={source.id}
   152→                className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[12px]"
   153→                style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   154→              >
   155→                {Icon && <Icon size={13} className="shrink-0" style={{ color: "#6b7280" }} />}
   156→                <span style={{ fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", color: "#374151" }}>
   157→                  {source.repository}
   158→                </span>
   159→                <span
   160→                  className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px]"
   161→                  style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}
   162→                >
   163→                  <span
   164→                    className="inline-block h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full"
   165→                    style={{ backgroundColor: source.enabled ? "#16a34a" : "#d1d5db" }}
   166→                  />
   167→                  {source.enabled ? "Active" : "Disabled"}
   168→                  <span className="ml-1" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   169→                    {source.poll_interval_seconds < 60
   170→                      ? `${source.poll_interval_seconds}s`
   171→                      : source.poll_interval_seconds < 3600
   172→                        ? `${Math.round(source.poll_interval_seconds / 60)}m`
   173→                        : `${(source.poll_interval_seconds / 3600).toFixed(1)}h`}
   174→                  </span>
   175→                </span>
   176→              </div>
   177→            );
   178→          })}
   179→        </div>
   180→      ) : (
   181→        <p className="text-[12px] italic" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   182→          No sources configured
   183→        </p>
   184→      )}
   185→
   186→      {adding && (
   187→        <InlineSourceForm projectId={projectId} onDone={() => setAdding(false)} />
   188→      )}
   189→    </div>
   190→  );
   191→}
   192→
   193→/* ---------- Recent Releases Section ---------- */
   194→
   195→function RecentReleasesSection({ projectId }: { projectId: string }) {
   196→  const { data: relData } = useSWR(`project-${projectId}-card-releases`, () =>

   213→      >

   215→      </span>
   216→
   217→      {releases.length > 0 ? (
   218→        <div className="space-y-1">

   234→                  }}

   245→                  {timeAgo(r.released_at || r.created_at)}
   246→                </span>
   247→              </Link>
   248→            );
   249→          })}
   250→          <Link
   251→            href={`/releases?project=${projectId}`}
   252→            className="block text-[11px] mt-1 hover:underline"

   267→/* ---------- Semantic Releases Section ---------- */
   268→
   269→const URGENCY_COLORS: Record<string, { bg: string; text: string }> = {

   271→  high: { bg: "#f97316", text: "#ffffff" },
   272→};
   273→
   274→function SemanticReleasesSection({ projectId }: { projectId: string }) {
   275→  const { data } = useSWR(`project-${projectId}-card-sr`, () =>
   276→    srApi.list(projectId, 1, 3)

   289→      {items.length > 0 ? (

   300→              >

   303→                  style={{
   304→                    backgroundColor: "#eff6ff",
   305→                    fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
   306→                    color: "#1d4ed8",
   307→                  }}
   308→                >
   309→                  {sr.version}
   310→                </span>
   311→                <span
   312→                  className="text-[11px]"
   313→                  style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}
   314→                >
   315→                  {sr.status}
   316→                </span>
   317→                {urgencyStyle && (
   318→                  <span
   319→                    className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium leading-none"
   320→                    style={{ backgroundColor: urgencyStyle.bg, color: urgencyStyle.text }}

   322→                    {sr.report!.urgency}
   323→                  </span>
   324→                )}
   325→                {sr.report?.summary && (

   333→                  </span>
   334→                )}
   335→                <span className="ml-auto text-[11px] shrink-0" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   336→                  {timeAgo(sr.completed_at || sr.created_at)}
   337→                </span>
   338→              </Link>
   339→            );
   340→          })}
   341→          <Link
   342→            href={`/projects/${projectId}/semantic-releases`}
   343→            className="block text-[11px] mt-1 hover:underline"
   344→            style={{ color: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   345→          >
   346→            View all &rarr;
   347→          </Link>
   348→        </div>
   349→      ) : (
   350→        <p className="text-[12px] italic" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   351→          No semantic releases yet
   352→        </p>
   353→      )}
   354→    </div>
   355→  );
   356→}
   357→
   358→/* ---------- Project Card ---------- */
   359→
   360→function ProjectCard({ project }: { project: Project }) {
   361→  return (
   362→    <div
   363→      className="overflow-hidden rounded-md"
   364→      style={{ border: "1px solid #e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#ffffff" }}
   365→    >
   366→      {/* Header */}
   367→      <div className="px-4 py-3">
   368→        <div className="min-w-0">
   369→          <Link
   370→            href={`/projects/${project.id}`}
   371→            className="group inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[16px] font-bold transition-colors"
   372→            style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)", color: "#e8601a" }}
   373→          >
   374→            {project.name}
   375→            <ArrowRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5 opacity-0 -translate-x-1 transition-all group-hover:opacity-100 group-hover:translate-x-0" />
   376→          </Link>
   377→          {project.description && (
   378→            <p
   379→              className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] truncate"
   380→              style={{ color: "#6b7280", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   381→            >
   382→              {project.description}
   383→            </p>
   384→          )}
   385→        </div>
   386→      </div>
   387→
   388→      {/* Content columns: sources | recent releases | semantic releases */}
   389→      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 border-t" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   390→        <SourcesSection projectId={project.id} />
   391→        <RecentReleasesSection projectId={project.id} />
   392→        <SemanticReleasesSection projectId={project.id} />
   393→      </div>
   394→    </div>
   395→  );
   396→}
   397→
   398→/* ---------- Page ---------- */
   399→
   400→export default function ProjectsPage() {
   401→  const [createOpen, setCreateOpen] = useState(false);
   402→  const { data, isLoading } = useSWR("projects", () => projectsApi.list());
   403→  const items = data?.data ?? [];
   404→
   405→  return (
   406→    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 fade-in">
   407→      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
   408→        <div>
   409→          <h1
   410→            style={{
   411→              fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
   412→              fontSize: "24px",
   413→              fontWeight: 700,
   414→              color: "#111113",
   415→            }}
   416→          >
   417→            Projects
   418→          </h1>
   419→          <p
   420→            className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-[#6b7280]"
   421→            style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   422→          >
   423→            Tracked software projects and their recent releases.
   424→          </p>
   425→        </div>
   426→        <button
   427→          onClick={() => setCreateOpen(true)}
   428→          className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] text-white transition-opacity hover:opacity-90"
   429→          style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   430→        >
   431→          <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   432→          New Project
   433→        </button>
   434→      </div>
   435→
   436→      {isLoading ? (
   437→        <p
   438→          className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-[14px] italic text-[#9ca3af]"
   439→          style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
   440→        >
   441→          Loading...
   442→        </p>
   443→      ) : items.length === 0 ? (
   444→        <div
   445→          className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-md border py-12"
   446→          style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#ffffff" }}
   447→        >
   448→          <p
   449→            className="text-[14px] italic text-[#9ca3af]"
   450→            style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
   451→          >
   452→            No projects yet — create one to start tracking releases
   453→          </p>
   454→          <button
   455→            onClick={() => setCreateOpen(true)}
   456→            className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] text-white transition-opacity hover:opacity-90"
   457→            style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   458→          >
   459→            <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   460→            New Project
   461→          </button>
   462→        </div>
   463→      ) : (
   464→        <div className="space-y-4">
   465→          {items.map((project) => (
   466→            <ProjectCard key={project.id} project={project} />
   467→          ))}
   468→        </div>
   469→      )}
   470→
   471→      <Dialog open={createOpen} onOpenChange={setCreateOpen}>
   472→        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-lg">
   473→          <DialogHeader>
   474→            <DialogTitle>Create Project</DialogTitle>
   475→          </DialogHeader>
   476→          <ProjectForm
   477→            title="Create Project"
   478→            onSubmit={async (result) => {
   479→              const created = await projectsApi.create(result.project);
   480→              if (result.source && created.data?.id) {
   481→                await sourcesApi.create(created.data.id, result.source);
   482→              }
   483→            }}
   484→            onSuccess={() => { setCreateOpen(false); mutate("projects"); }}
   485→            onCancel={() => setCreateOpen(false)}
   486→          />
   487→        </DialogContent>
   488→      </Dialog>
   489→    </div>
   490→  );
   491→}

    21→  _projectId?: string;

    25→
    26→const PER_PAGE = 15;
    27→
    28→function getProviderUrl(
    29→  provider: string,
    30→  repository: string,
    31→  version: string
    32→): string | null {
    33→  switch (provider) {
    34→    case "github":
    35→      return `https://github.com/${repository}/releases/tag/${version}`;
    36→    case "dockerhub":
    37→      return `https://hub.docker.com/r/${repository}/tags?name=${encodeURIComponent(version)}`;
    38→    default:
    39→      return null;
    40→  }
    41→}
    42→
    43→/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
    44→/*  Page                                                               */
    45→/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
    46→
    47→export default function ReleasesPage() {
    48→  return (
    49→    <Suspense>
    50→      <ReleasesPageInner />
    51→    </Suspense>
    52→  );
    53→}
    54→
    55→function ReleasesPageInner() {
    56→  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
    57→  const initialProject = searchParams.get("project") ?? "all";
    58→  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
    59→  const [projectFilter, setProjectFilter] = useState<string>(initialProject);
    60→
    61→  /* Fetch projects for the filter dropdown + source enrichment */
    62→  const { data: projectsData } = useSWR("projects-for-filter", () =>
    63→    projectsApi.list()
    64→  );
    65→
    66→  /* Build a map: sourceId -> { provider, repository, projectName, projectId } */
    67→  const { data: sourceMap } = useSWR(
    68→    projectsData ? "source-map-for-releases" : null,
    69→    async () => {
    70→      if (!projectsData?.data?.length) return new Map<string, { provider: string; repository: string; projectName: string; projectId: string }>();
    71→      const map = new Map<string, { provider: string; repository: string; projectName: string; projectId: string }>();
    72→      await Promise.all(
    73→        projectsData.data.map(async (p: Project) => {
    74→          const res = await sourcesApi.listByProject(p.id).catch(() => null);
    75→          if (res?.data) {
    76→            for (const s of res.data) {
    77→              map.set(s.id, {
    78→                provider: s.provider,
    79→                repository: s.repository,
    80→                projectName: p.name,
    81→                projectId: p.id,
    82→              });
    83→            }
    84→          }
    85→        })
    86→      );
    87→      return map;
    88→    }
    89→  );
    90→
    91→  /* Fetch releases — scoped by project or all */
    92→  const { data: scopedData, isLoading: scopedLoading } = useSWR(
    93→    projectFilter !== "all" ? ["releases", page, projectFilter] : null,
    94→    () => releasesApi.listByProject(projectFilter, page, PER_PAGE)
    95→  );
    96→
    97→  const { data: allReleasesData, isLoading: allLoading } = useSWR(
    98→    projectFilter === "all" ? ["all-releases", page] : null,
    99→    () => releasesApi.list(page, PER_PAGE)
   100→  );
   101→
   102→  const isLoading = projectFilter !== "all" ? scopedLoading : allLoading;
   103→
   104→  /* Enrich releases with source metadata */
   105→  const rawReleases: Release[] =
   106→    projectFilter !== "all"
   107→      ? scopedData?.data ?? []
   108→      : allReleasesData?.data ?? [];
   109→
   110→  const releases: ReleaseRow[] = rawReleases.map((r) => {
   111→    const info = sourceMap?.get(r.source_id);
   112→    return {
   113→      ...r,
   114→      _projectName: info?.projectName,
   115→      _projectId: info?.projectId,
   116→      _provider: info?.provider,
   117→      _repository: info?.repository,
   118→    };
   119→  });
   120→
   121→  /* Pagination math */
   122→  const activeData = projectFilter !== "all" ? scopedData : allReleasesData;
   123→  const total = activeData?.meta?.total ?? 0;
   124→  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / PER_PAGE));
   125→  const startRow = (page - 1) * PER_PAGE + 1;
   126→  const endRow = Math.min(page * PER_PAGE, total);
   127→
   128→  return (
   129→    <div className="space-y-6">
   130→      {/* Page title */}
   131→      <h1
   132→        style={{
   133→          fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
   134→          fontSize: "24px",
   135→          fontWeight: 700,
   136→          color: "#111113",
   137→        }}
   138→      >
   139→        Releases
   140→      </h1>
   141→
   142→      {/* Project filter */}
   143→      <div>
   144→        <select
   145→          value={projectFilter}
   146→          onChange={(e) => {
   147→            setProjectFilter(e.target.value);
   148→            setPage(1);
   149→          }}
   150→          className="appearance-none rounded-md bg-white px-3 py-2 pr-8 outline-none transition-shadow"
   151→          style={{
   152→            fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   153→            fontSize: "13px",
   154→            color: "#111113",
   155→            border: "1px solid #e8e8e5",
   156→          }}
   157→          onFocus={(e) =>
   158→            (e.currentTarget.style.boxShadow = "0 0 0 2px #e8601a40")
   159→          }
   160→          onBlur={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.boxShadow = "none")}
   161→        >
   162→          <option value="all">All Projects</option>
   163→          {projectsData?.data.map((p: Project) => (
   164→            <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>
   165→              {p.name}
   166→            </option>
   167→          ))}
   168→        </select>
   169→      </div>
   170→
   171→      {/* Table card */}
   172→      <div
   173→        className="overflow-hidden rounded-lg bg-white"
   174→        style={{ border: "1px solid #e8e8e5" }}
   175→      >
   176→        {isLoading ? (
   177→          <div
   178→            className="py-16 text-center"
   179→            style={{
   180→              fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   181→              fontSize: "13px",
   182→              color: "#6b7280",
   183→            }}
   184→          >
   185→            Loading...
   186→          </div>
   187→        ) : releases.length === 0 ? (
   188→          <div className="py-16 text-center">
   189→            <p
   190→              style={{
   191→                fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
   192→                fontStyle: "italic",
   193→                fontSize: "15px",
   194→                color: "#9ca3af",
   195→              }}
   196→            >
   197→              No releases ingested yet

   223→            </thead>

   230→                >

   232→                  <td className="px-4 py-3">

    10→// ReleasesStore defines the persistence operations for releases (read-only).
    11→type ReleasesStore interface {
    12→	ListAllReleases(ctx context.Context, page, perPage int) ([]models.Release, int, error)
    13→	ListReleasesBySource(ctx context.Context, sourceID string, page, perPage int) ([]models.Release, int, error)
    14→	ListReleasesByProject(ctx context.Context, projectID string, page, perPage int) ([]models.Release, int, error)
    15→	GetRelease(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.Release, error)
    16→}
    17→
    18→// ReleasesHandler implements HTTP handlers for the /releases resource.
    19→type ReleasesHandler struct {
    20→	store ReleasesStore
    21→}
    22→
    23→// NewReleasesHandler returns a new ReleasesHandler.
    24→func NewReleasesHandler(store ReleasesStore) *ReleasesHandler {
    25→	return &ReleasesHandler{store: store}
    26→}
    27→
    28→// List handles GET /releases — returns all releases across all projects.
    29→func (h *ReleasesHandler) List(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    30→	page, perPage := ParsePagination(r)
    31→	releases, total, err := h.store.ListAllReleases(r.Context(), page, perPage)
    32→	if err != nil {
    33→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal_error", "Failed to list releases")
    34→		return
    35→	}
    36→	if releases == nil {
    37→		releases = []models.Release{}
    38→	}
    39→	RespondList(w, r, http.StatusOK, releases, page, perPage, total)
    40→}
    41→
    42→// ListBySource handles GET /sources/{id}/releases — returns releases for a specific source.
    43→func (h *ReleasesHandler) ListBySource(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    44→	sourceID := r.PathValue("id")
    45→	if sourceID == "" {
    46→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Source ID is required")
    47→		return
    48→	}
    49→	page, perPage := ParsePagination(r)
    50→	releases, total, err := h.store.ListReleasesBySource(r.Context(), sourceID, page, perPage)
    51→	if err != nil {
    52→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal_error", "Failed to list releases")
    53→		return
    54→	}
    55→	if releases == nil {
    56→		releases = []models.Release{}
    57→	}
    58→	RespondList(w, r, http.StatusOK, releases, page, perPage, total)
    59→}
    60→
    61→// ListByProject handles GET /projects/{projectId}/releases — returns releases for all sources in a project.
    62→func (h *ReleasesHandler) ListByProject(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    63→	projectID := r.PathValue("projectId")
    64→	if projectID == "" {
    65→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Project ID is required")
    66→		return
    67→	}
    68→	page, perPage := ParsePagination(r)
    69→	releases, total, err := h.store.ListReleasesByProject(r.Context(), projectID, page, perPage)
    70→	if err != nil {
    71→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal_error", "Failed to list releases")
    72→		return
    73→	}
    74→	if releases == nil {
    75→		releases = []models.Release{}
    76→	}
    77→	RespondList(w, r, http.StatusOK, releases, page, perPage, total)
    78→}
    79→
    80→// Get handles GET /releases/{id} — returns a single release.
    81→func (h *ReleasesHandler) Get(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    82→	id := r.PathValue("id")
    83→	if id == "" {
    84→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Release ID is required")
    85→		return
    86→	}
    87→	rel, err := h.store.GetRelease(r.Context(), id)
    88→	if err != nil {
    89→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusNotFound, "not_found", "Release not found")
    90→		return
    91→	}
    92→	RespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusOK, rel)
    93→}
    94→

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/pgstore.go",
  "old_string": "// --- SemanticReleasesStore ---\n\nfunc (s *PgStore) ListSemanticReleases",
  "new_string": "// --- SemanticReleasesStore ---\n\nfunc (s *PgStore) ListAllSemanticReleases(ctx context.Context, page, perPage int) ([]models.SemanticRelease, int, error) {\n\tvar total int\n\terr := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM semantic_releases`).Scan(&total)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, 0, fmt.Errorf(\"count semantic releases: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\toffset := (page - 1) * perPage\n\trows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT id, project_id, version, COALESCE(report,'{}'), status, COALESCE(error,''),\n\t\t        created_at, completed_at\n\t\t FROM semantic_releases ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT $1 OFFSET $2`, perPage, offset)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, 0, fmt.Errorf(\"list all semantic releases: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer rows.Close()\n\tvar releases []models.SemanticRelease\n\tfor rows.Next() {\n\t\tvar sr models.SemanticRelease\n\t\tif err := rows.Scan(&sr.ID, &sr.ProjectID, &sr.Version, &sr.Report, &sr.Status, &sr.Error,\n\t\t\t&sr.CreatedAt, &sr.CompletedAt); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, 0, fmt.Errorf(\"scan semantic release: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\treleases = append(releases, sr)\n\t}\n\treturn releases, total, nil\n}\n\nfunc (s *PgStore) ListSemanticReleases"
}
