{
  "file_path": "/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/semantic-releases/semantic-releases-list.tsx",
  "content": "\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState, Suspense } from \"react\";\nimport { useSearchParams } from \"next/navigation\";\nimport useSWR from \"swr\";\nimport Link from \"next/link\";\nimport {\n  semanticReleases as srApi,\n  projects as projectsApi,\n} from \"@/lib/api/client\";\nimport { VersionChip } from \"@/components/ui/version-chip\";\nimport { ConfirmDialog } from \"@/components/ui/confirm-dialog\";\nimport { Trash2 } from \"lucide-react\";\nimport { timeAgo } from \"@/lib/format\";\nimport type { Project, SemanticRelease } from \"@/lib/api/types\";\n\n/* ---------- Urgency chip ---------- */\n\nconst URGENCY_COLORS: Record<string, { bg: string; text: string }> = {\n  critical: { bg: \"#dc2626\", text: \"#ffffff\" },\n  high: { bg: \"#f97316\", text: \"#ffffff\" },\n};\n\nfunction UrgencyChip({ urgency }: { urgency: string }) {\n  const u = urgency.toLowerCase();\n  const style = URGENCY_COLORS[u];\n  if (!style) return null;\n  return (\n    <span\n      className=\"inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-medium leading-none\"\n      style={{ backgroundColor: style.bg, color: style.text }}\n    >\n      {urgency}\n    </span>\n  );\n}\n\n/* ---------- Helpers ---------- */\n\nfunction truncate(str: string, max: number): string {\n  return str.length > max ? str.slice(0, max) + \"\\u2026\" : str;\n}\n\nconst PER_PAGE = 15;\n\n/* ---------- Main component ---------- */\n\nexport function SemanticReleasesList() {\n  return (\n    <Suspense>\n      <SemanticReleasesListInner />\n    </Suspense>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction SemanticReleasesListInner() {\n  const searchParams = useSearchParams();\n  const initialProject = searchParams.get(\"project\") ?? \"all\";\n  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);\n  const [projectFilter, setProjectFilter] = useState<string>(initialProject);\n  const [deletingId, setDeletingId] = useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  /* Fetch projects for the filter dropdown */\n  const { data: projectsData } = useSWR(\"projects-for-sr-filter\", () =>\n    projectsApi.list()\n  );\n  const projectsById = new Map<string, Project>();\n  for (const p of projectsData?.data ?? []) projectsById.set(p.id, p);\n\n  /* Fetch semantic releases — scoped by project or all */\n  const { data: scopedData, isLoading: scopedLoading, mutate: mutateScopedData } = useSWR(\n    projectFilter !== \"all\" ? [\"sr-scoped\", page, projectFilter] : null,\n    () => srApi.list(projectFilter, page, PER_PAGE)\n  );\n\n  const { data: allData, isLoading: allLoading, mutate: mutateAllData } = useSWR(\n    projectFilter === \"all\" ? [\"sr-all\", page] : null,\n    () => srApi.listAll(page, PER_PAGE)\n  );\n\n  const isLoading = projectFilter !== \"all\" ? scopedLoading : allLoading;\n  const releases: SemanticRelease[] =\n    projectFilter !== \"all\"\n      ? scopedData?.data ?? []\n      : allData?.data ?? [];\n\n  const mutateData = projectFilter !== \"all\" ? mutateScopedData : mutateAllData;\n\n  /* Pagination math */\n  const activeData = projectFilter !== \"all\" ? scopedData : allData;\n  const total = activeData?.meta?.total ?? 0;\n  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / PER_PAGE));\n  const startRow = (page - 1) * PER_PAGE + 1;\n  const endRow = Math.min(page * PER_PAGE, total);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"space-y-6 fade-in\">\n      {/* Page title */}\n      <h1\n        style={{\n          fontFamily: \"var(--font-fraunces)\",\n          fontSize: \"24px\",\n          fontWeight: 700,\n          color: \"#111113\",\n        }}\n      >\n        Semantic Releases\n      </h1>\n\n      {/* Project filter */}\n      <div>\n        <select\n          value={projectFilter}\n          onChange={(e) => {\n            setProjectFilter(e.target.value);\n            setPage(1);\n          }}\n          className=\"appearance-none rounded-md bg-white px-3 py-2 pr-8 outline-none transition-shadow\"\n          style={{\n            fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n            fontSize: \"13px\",\n            color: \"#111113\",\n            border: \"1px solid #e8e8e5\",\n          }}\n          onFocus={(e) =>\n            (e.currentTarget.style.boxShadow = \"0 0 0 2px #e8601a40\")\n          }\n          onBlur={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.boxShadow = \"none\")}\n        >\n          <option value=\"all\">All Projects</option>\n          {projectsData?.data.map((p: Project) => (\n            <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>\n              {p.name}\n            </option>\n          ))}\n        </select>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Table card */}\n      <div\n        className=\"overflow-hidden rounded-lg bg-white\"\n        style={{ border: \"1px solid #e8e8e5\" }}\n      >\n        {isLoading ? (\n          <div\n            className=\"py-16 text-center\"\n            style={{\n              fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n              fontSize: \"13px\",\n              color: \"#6b7280\",\n            }}\n          >\n            Loading...\n          </div>\n        ) : releases.length === 0 ? (\n          <div className=\"py-16 text-center\">\n            <p\n              style={{\n                fontFamily: \"var(--font-fraunces)\",\n                fontStyle: \"italic\",\n                fontSize: \"15px\",\n                color: \"#9ca3af\",\n              }}\n            >\n              No semantic releases yet\n            </p>\n          </div>\n        ) : (\n          <table className=\"w-full\">\n            <thead>\n              <tr style={{ borderBottom: \"1px solid #e8e8e5\", backgroundColor: \"#fafaf9\" }}>\n                {[\"Project\", \"Version\", \"Status\", \"Urgency\", \"Age\", \"\"].map(\n                  (col) => (\n                    <th\n                      key={col}\n                      className=\"px-4 py-3 text-left\"\n                      style={{\n                        fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n                        fontSize: \"11px\",\n                        fontWeight: 600,\n                        textTransform: \"uppercase\" as const,\n                        letterSpacing: \"0.08em\",\n                        color: \"#9ca3af\",\n                      }}\n                    >\n                      {col}\n                    </th>\n                  )\n                )}\n              </tr>\n            </thead>\n            <tbody>\n              {releases.map((sr) => {\n                const project = projectsById.get(sr.project_id);\n                return (\n                  <tr\n                    key={sr.id}\n                    className=\"transition-colors hover:bg-[#fafaf9]\"\n                    style={{ borderBottom: \"1px solid #e8e8e5\" }}\n                  >\n                    {/* Project */}\n                    <td className=\"px-4 py-3\">\n                      {project ? (\n                        <Link\n                          href={`/projects/${project.id}`}\n                          className=\"hover:underline\"\n                          style={{\n                            fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n                            fontSize: \"13px\",\n                            color: \"#111113\",\n                            fontWeight: 500,\n                          }}\n                        >\n                          {project.name}\n                        </Link>\n                      ) : (\n                        <span\n                          style={{\n                            fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n                            fontSize: \"13px\",\n                            color: \"#9ca3af\",\n                          }}\n                        >\n                          {\"\\u2014\"}\n                        </span>\n                      )}\n                    </td>\n\n                    {/* Version */}\n                    <td className=\"px-4 py-3\">\n                      <Link href={`/projects/${sr.project_id}/semantic-releases/${sr.id}`}>\n                        <VersionChip version={sr.version} />\n                      </Link>\n                    </td>\n\n                    {/* Status */}\n                    <td className=\"px-4 py-3\">\n                      <span\n                        className=\"text-[13px] font-medium\"\n                        style={{\n                          fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n                          color: sr.status === \"completed\" ? \"#16a34a\" : \"#e8601a\",\n                        }}\n                      >\n                        {sr.status}\n                      </span>\n                    </td>\n\n                    {/* Urgency */}\n                    <td className=\"px-4 py-3\">\n                      {sr.report?.urgency ? (\n                        <UrgencyChip urgency={sr.report.urgency} />\n                      ) : (\n                        <span\n                          style={{\n                            fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n                            fontSize: \"13px\",\n                            color: \"#9ca3af\",\n                          }}\n                        >\n                          {\"\\u2014\"}\n                        </span>\n                      )}\n                    </td>\n\n                    {/* Age */}\n                    <td className=\"px-4 py-3\">\n                      <span\n                        style={{\n                          fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n                          fontSize: \"13px\",\n                          color: \"#9ca3af\",\n                        }}\n                      >\n                        {timeAgo(sr.completed_at ?? sr.created_at)}\n                      </span>\n                    </td>\n\n                    {/* Delete */}\n                    <td className=\"px-4 py-3\">\n                      <button\n                        onClick={() => setDeletingId(sr.id)}\n                        className=\"rounded p-1 transition-colors hover:bg-red-50 hover:text-red-600\"\n                        style={{ color: \"#9ca3af\" }}\n                        title=\"Delete semantic release\"\n                      >\n                        <Trash2 className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n                      </button>\n                    </td>\n                  </tr>\n                );\n              })}\n            </tbody>\n          </table>\n        )}\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Pagination */}\n      {total > 0 && (\n        <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n          <span\n            style={{\n              fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n              fontSize: \"13px\",\n              color: \"#9ca3af\",\n            }}\n          >\n            {startRow}&ndash;{endRow} of {total}\n          </span>\n          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n            <button\n              disabled={page <= 1}\n              onClick={() => setPage(page - 1)}\n              className=\"rounded-md bg-white px-3 py-1.5 transition-colors hover:bg-[#fafaf9] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40\"\n              style={{\n                fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n                fontSize: \"13px\",\n                color: \"#374151\",\n                border: \"1px solid #e8e8e5\",\n              }}\n            >\n              Previous\n            </button>\n            <button\n              disabled={page >= totalPages}\n              onClick={() => setPage(page + 1)}\n              className=\"rounded-md bg-white px-3 py-1.5 transition-colors hover:bg-[#fafaf9] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40\"\n              style={{\n                fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n                fontSize: \"13px\",\n                color: \"#374151\",\n                border: \"1px solid #e8e8e5\",\n              }}\n            >\n              Next\n            </button>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Delete confirmation */}\n      <ConfirmDialog\n        open={!!deletingId}\n        onOpenChange={(open) => {\n          if (!open) setDeletingId(null);\n        }}\n        title=\"Delete Semantic Release\"\n        description=\"This will permanently delete this semantic release and its report.\"\n        onConfirm={async () => {\n          if (deletingId) {\n            await srApi.delete(deletingId);\n            mutateData();\n          }\n        }}\n      />\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

**Backend (Go):**

6. `web/app/semantic-releases/page.tsx` — New top-level route page (thin wrapper)

11. `web/app/projects/[id]/semantic-releases/page.tsx` — Deleted (detail route at `[srId]` preserved)

**Verification:** `go test ./internal/api/...` passes, `npx tsc --noEmit` passes with no errors.

     2→"use client";
     3→
     4→import useSWR from "swr";
     5→import Link from "next/link";
     6→import { StatsCards } from "@/components/dashboard/stats-cards";
     7→import { RecentReleases } from "@/components/dashboard/recent-releases";
     8→import {
     9→  projects as projectsApi,
    10→  semanticReleases as srApi,
    11→} from "@/lib/api/client";
    12→import { StatusDot } from "@/components/ui/status-dot";
    13→import { VersionChip } from "@/components/ui/version-chip";
    14→import type { SemanticRelease } from "@/lib/api/types";
    15→import { timeAgo } from "@/lib/format";
    16→
    17→interface SRRow extends SemanticRelease {
    18→  _projectName?: string;
    19→}
    20→
    21→function SemanticReleasesColumn() {
    22→  const { data: projectsData } = useSWR("projects-for-sr-dashboard", () =>
    23→    projectsApi.list()
    24→  );
    25→
    26→  const { data: allSRs, isLoading } = useSWR(
    27→    projectsData ? "recent-semantic-releases" : null,
    28→    async () => {
    29→      if (!projectsData?.data?.length) return [];
    30→
    31→      const projectMap = new Map(
    32→        projectsData.data.map((p) => [p.id, p.name])
    33→      );
    34→
    35→      const results = await Promise.all(
    36→        projectsData.data.slice(0, 10).map((p) =>
    37→          srApi.list(p.id, 1).catch(() => null)
    38→        )
    39→      );
    40→
    41→      const srs: SRRow[] = results
    42→        .filter((r): r is NonNullable<typeof r> => r !== null)
    43→        .flatMap((r) => r.data)
    44→        .sort(
    45→          (a, b) =>
    46→            new Date(b.created_at).getTime() - new Date(a.created_at).getTime()
    47→        )
    48→        .slice(0, 6)
    49→        .map((sr) => ({
    50→          ...sr,
    51→          _projectName: projectMap.get(sr.project_id),
    52→        }));
    53→
    54→      return srs;
    55→    }
    56→  );
    57→
    58→  return (
    59→    <div
    60→      className="rounded-lg bg-white"
    61→      style={{ border: "1px solid #e8e8e5" }}
    62→    >
    63→      {/* Header */}
    64→      <div
    65→        className="flex items-center justify-between px-5 py-4"
    66→        style={{ borderBottom: "1px solid #e8e8e5" }}
    67→      >
    68→        <h3
    69→          className="font-medium"
    70→          style={{
    71→            fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
    72→            fontSize: "13px",
    73→            color: "#111113",
    74→          }}
    75→        >
    76→          Semantic Releases
    77→        </h3>
    78→        <Link
    79→          href="/projects"
    80→          className="text-sm hover:underline"
    81→          style={{
    82→            fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
    83→            fontSize: "13px",
    84→            color: "#e8601a",
    85→          }}
    86→        >
    87→          View all &rarr;
    88→        </Link>
    89→      </div>
    90→
    91→      {/* Body */}
    92→      <div className="p-4 space-y-3">
    93→        {isLoading ? (
    94→          <div
    95→            className="py-8 text-center"
    96→            style={{
    97→              fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
    98→              fontSize: "13px",
    99→              color: "#6b7280",
   100→            }}
   101→          >
   102→            Loading...
   103→          </div>
   104→        ) : allSRs && allSRs.length > 0 ? (
   105→          allSRs.map((sr: SRRow) => (
   106→            <Link
   107→              key={sr.id}
   108→              href={`/projects/${sr.project_id}/semantic-releases/${sr.id}`}
   109→              className="block rounded-lg px-4 py-3 transition-colors hover:bg-[#fafaf9]"
   110→              style={{ border: "1px solid #e8e8e5" }}
   111→            >
   112→              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
   113→                <span
   114→                  className="font-semibold"
   115→                  style={{
   116→                    fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
   117→                    fontSize: "15px",
   118→                    color: "#111113",
   119→                  }}
   120→                >
   121→                  {sr._projectName ?? "Unknown Project"}
   122→                </span>
   123→                <VersionChip version={sr.version} />
   124→              </div>
   125→              {sr.report?.summary && (
   126→                <p
   127→                  className="mt-1 line-clamp-1"
   128→                  style={{
   129→                    fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   130→                    fontStyle: "italic",
   131→                    fontSize: "13px",
   132→                    color: "#6b7280",
   133→                  }}
   134→                >
   135→                  {sr.report.summary}
   136→                </p>
   137→              )}
   138→              <div className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-2">
   139→                <StatusDot status={sr.status} />
   140→                <span
   141→                  style={{
   142→                    fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   143→                    fontSize: "12px",
   144→                    color: "#9ca3af",
   145→                  }}
   146→                >
   147→                  {timeAgo(sr.created_at)}
   148→                </span>
   149→              </div>
   150→            </Link>
   151→          ))
   152→        ) : (
   153→          <div className="py-8 text-center">
   154→            <p
   155→              style={{
   156→                fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
   157→                fontStyle: "italic",
   158→                fontSize: "14px",
   159→                color: "#9ca3af",
   160→              }}
   161→            >
   162→              No semantic releases yet
   163→            </p>
   164→          </div>
   165→        )}
   166→      </div>
   167→    </div>
   168→  );
   169→}
   170→
   171→export default function DashboardPage() {
   172→  return (
   173→    <div className="space-y-6">
   174→      <h1
   175→        style={{
   176→          fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
   177→          fontSize: "24px",
   178→          fontWeight: 700,
   179→          color: "#111113",
   180→        }}
   181→      >
   182→        Dashboard
   183→      </h1>
   184→      <StatsCards />
   185→      <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-2">
   186→        <RecentReleases />
   187→        <SemanticReleasesColumn />
   188→      </div>
   189→    </div>
   190→  );
   191→}

    10→  contextSources as ctxApi,
    11→  agent as agentApi,
    12→} from "@/lib/api/client";

    23→/* ---------- Tabs ---------- */
    24→
    25→const tabs = [
    26→  { key: "sources", label: "Sources" },
    27→  { key: "context", label: "Context Sources" },
    28→  { key: "agent", label: "Agent" },
    29→] as const;
    30→
    31→type TabKey = (typeof tabs)[number]["key"];
    32→
    33→/* ---------- Helpers ---------- */
    34→
    35→function formatDuration(startedAt?: string, completedAt?: string): string {
    36→  if (!startedAt) return "--";
    37→  const start = new Date(startedAt).getTime();
    38→  const end = completedAt ? new Date(completedAt).getTime() : Date.now();
    39→  const secs = Math.round((end - start) / 1000);
    40→  if (secs < 60) return `${secs}s`;
    41→  const mins = Math.floor(secs / 60);
    42→  const rem = secs % 60;
    43→  return `${mins}m ${rem}s`;
    44→}
    45→
    46→function truncate(str: string, max: number): string {
    47→  return str.length > max ? str.slice(0, max) + "\u2026" : str;
    48→}
    49→
    50→function formatInterval(seconds: number): string {
    51→  if (seconds < 60) return `${seconds}s`;
    52→  if (seconds < 3600) return `${Math.round(seconds / 60)}m`;
    53→  return `${(seconds / 3600).toFixed(1)}h`;
    54→}
    55→
    56→/* ---------- Main component ---------- */
    57→
    58→export function ProjectDetail({ id }: { id: string }) {
    59→  const router = useRouter();
    60→  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<TabKey>("sources");
    61→  const [triggering, setTriggering] = useState(false);
    62→
    63→  /* Agent config local state */
    64→  const [promptDraft, setPromptDraft] = useState<string | null>(null);
    65→  const [rulesDraft, setRulesDraft] = useState<AgentRules | null>(null);
    66→  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
    67→
    68→  /* Inline edit state */

    70→  const [editingDesc, setEditingDesc] = useState(false);
    71→  const [nameDraft, setNameDraft] = useState("");
    72→  const [descDraft, setDescDraft] = useState("");
    73→  const nameRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
    74→  const descRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
    75→
    76→  /* Dialog state */
    77→  const [sourceCreateOpen, setSourceCreateOpen] = useState(false);
    78→  const [editingSource, setEditingSource] = useState<Source | null>(null);
    79→  const [deletingSourceId, setDeletingSourceId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    80→  const [ctxCreateOpen, setCtxCreateOpen] = useState(false);
    81→  const [deletingCtxId, setDeletingCtxId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    82→  const [deletingProject, setDeletingProject] = useState(false);
    83→
    84→  /* Data fetching */
    85→  const { data, isLoading, mutate: mutateProject } = useSWR(`project-${id}`, () => projectsApi.get(id));
    86→  const { data: sourcesData, mutate: mutateSources } = useSWR(
    87→    activeTab === "sources" ? `project-${id}-sources` : null,
    88→    () => sourcesApi.listByProject(id),
    89→  );
    90→  const { data: ctxData, mutate: mutateCtx } = useSWR(
    91→    activeTab === "context" ? `project-${id}-ctx` : null,
    92→    () => ctxApi.list(id),
    93→  );
    94→  const { data: runsData, mutate: mutateRuns } = useSWR(
    95→    activeTab === "agent" ? `project-${id}-runs` : null,
    96→    () => agentApi.listRuns(id),
    97→  );
    98→
    99→  const saveName = useCallback(async () => {
   100→    const p = data?.data;
   101→    if (!p) return;
   102→    const trimmed = nameDraft.trim();
   103→    if (trimmed && trimmed !== p.name) {
   104→      await projectsApi.update(id, { name: trimmed, description: p.description });
   105→      mutateProject();
   106→    }
   107→    setEditingName(false);
   108→  }, [nameDraft, data, id, mutateProject]);
   109→
   110→  const saveDesc = useCallback(async () => {
   111→    const p = data?.data;
   112→    if (!p) return;
   113→    const trimmed = descDraft.trim();
   114→    if (trimmed !== (p.description ?? "")) {
   115→      await projectsApi.update(id, { name: p.name, description: trimmed || undefined });
   116→      mutateProject();
   117→    }
   118→    setEditingDesc(false);
   119→  }, [descDraft, data, id, mutateProject]);
   120→
   121→  /* Click-outside handlers */
   122→  useEffect(() => {
   123→    if (!editingName) return;
   124→    const handler = (e: MouseEvent) => {
   125→      if (nameRef.current && !nameRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) saveName();
   126→    };
   127→    document.addEventListener("mousedown", handler);
   128→    return () => document.removeEventListener("mousedown", handler);
   129→  }, [editingName, saveName]);
   130→
   131→  useEffect(() => {
   132→    if (!editingDesc) return;
   133→    const handler = (e: MouseEvent) => {
   134→      if (descRef.current && !descRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) saveDesc();
   135→    };
   136→    document.addEventListener("mousedown", handler);
   137→    return () => document.removeEventListener("mousedown", handler);
   138→  }, [editingDesc, saveDesc]);
   139→
   140→  /* Handlers */
   141→  const handleTriggerRun = async () => {
   142→    setTriggering(true);
   143→    try {
   144→      await agentApi.triggerRun(id);
   145→      mutateRuns();
   146→    } finally {
   147→      setTriggering(false);
   148→    }
   149→  };
   150→
   151→  const handleToggleSource = async (source: { id: string; provider: string; repository: string; poll_interval_seconds: number; enabled: boolean; config?: Record<string, unknown> }) => {
   152→    await sourcesApi.update(source.id, {
   153→      provider: source.provider,
   154→      repository: source.repository,
   155→      poll_interval_seconds: source.poll_interval_seconds,
   156→      enabled: !source.enabled,
   157→      config: source.config,
   158→    });
   159→    mutateSources();
   160→  };
   161→
   162→  const handleSaveAgentConfig = async () => {
   163→    if (!project) return;
   164→    setSaving(true);
   165→    try {
   166→      const input = {
   167→        name: project.name,
   168→        description: project.description,
   169→        agent_prompt: promptDraft ?? project.agent_prompt,
   170→        agent_rules: rulesDraft ?? project.agent_rules,
   171→      };
   172→      await projectsApi.update(id, input);
   173→      mutateProject();
   174→      setPromptDraft(null);
   175→      setRulesDraft(null);
   176→    } finally {
   177→      setSaving(false);
   178→    }
   179→  };
   180→
   181→  /* Loading / not found states */
   182→  if (isLoading) {
   183→    return (
   184→      <div className="flex h-48 items-center justify-center" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   185→        Loading...
   186→      </div>
   187→    );
   188→  }
   189→
   190→  const project = data?.data;
   191→  if (!project) {
   192→    return (
   193→      <div className="flex h-48 items-center justify-center" style={{ color: "#6b7280" }}>
   194→        Project not found
   195→      </div>
   196→    );
   197→  }
   198→
   199→  /* Derived counts */
   200→  const sourceCount = sourcesData?.data?.length;
   201→  const ctxCount = ctxData?.data?.length;
   202→
   203→  /* Current effective values */
   204→  const currentPrompt = promptDraft ?? project.agent_prompt ?? "";
   205→  const currentRules: AgentRules = rulesDraft ?? project.agent_rules ?? {};
   206→
   207→  return (
   208→    <div className="space-y-0">
   209→      {/* ===== Header zone ===== */}
   210→      <div
   211→        className="-m-6 mb-0 border-b px-6 py-5"
   212→        style={{ backgroundColor: "#ffffff", borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}
   213→      >
   214→        {/* Back link */}
   215→        <Link
   216→          href="/projects"
   217→          className="mb-4 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors hover:opacity-70"
   218→          style={{
   219→            fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   220→            fontSize: "13px",
   221→            color: "#6b7280",
   222→          }}
   223→        >
   224→          <ArrowLeft size={14} />
   225→          Back to Projects
   226→        </Link>
   227→        <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
   228→          {/* Left: project info */}
   229→          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
   230→            {editingName ? (
   231→              <input
   232→                ref={nameRef}
   233→                value={nameDraft}
   234→                onChange={(e) => setNameDraft(e.target.value)}
   235→                onKeyDown={(e) => {
   236→                  if (e.key === "Enter") saveName();
   237→                  if (e.key === "Escape") setEditingName(false);
   238→                }}
   239→                autoFocus
   240→                className="w-full max-w-md rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[28px] font-bold leading-tight focus:outline-none focus:ring-1"
   241→                style={{
   242→                  fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces), serif",
   243→                  borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   244→                  color: "#111113",
   245→                }}
   246→              />
   247→            ) : (
   248→              <h1
   249→                className="group flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 text-[28px] font-bold leading-tight"
   250→                style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces), serif" }}
   251→                onClick={() => {
   252→                  setNameDraft(project.name);
   253→                  setEditingName(true);
   254→                }}
   255→              >
   256→                {project.name}
   257→                <Pencil className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-40" />
   258→              </h1>
   259→            )}
   260→            {editingDesc ? (
   261→              <input
   262→                ref={descRef}
   263→                value={descDraft}
   264→                onChange={(e) => setDescDraft(e.target.value)}
   265→                onKeyDown={(e) => {
   266→                  if (e.key === "Enter") saveDesc();
   267→                  if (e.key === "Escape") setEditingDesc(false);
   268→                }}
   269→                autoFocus
   270→                placeholder="Add a description..."
   271→                className="mt-1 w-full max-w-lg rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[14px] focus:outline-none focus:ring-1"
   272→                style={{
   273→                  fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif",
   274→                  borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   275→                  color: "#6b7280",
   276→                }}
   277→              />
   278→            ) : (
   279→              <p
   280→                className="group mt-1 flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 text-[14px]"
   281→                style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif", color: "#6b7280" }}
   282→                onClick={() => {
   283→                  setDescDraft(project.description ?? "");
   284→                  setEditingDesc(true);
   285→                }}
   286→              >
   287→                {project.description || "Add a description..."}
   288→                <Pencil className="h-3 w-3 shrink-0 opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-40" />
   289→              </p>
   290→            )}
   291→            <p
   292→              className="mt-2 text-[12px]"
   293→              style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif", color: "#9ca3af" }}
   294→            >
   295→              {sourceCount !== undefined ? `${sourceCount} sources` : "-- sources"}
   296→              {" \u00B7 "}
   297→              {ctxCount !== undefined ? `${ctxCount} context sources` : "-- context sources"}
   298→            </p>
   299→          </div>
   300→
   301→          {/* Right: actions */}
   302→          <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2 ml-4">
   303→            <button
   304→              onClick={() => setDeletingProject(true)}
   305→              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] font-medium transition-colors hover:bg-red-50"
   306→              style={{
   307→                fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif",
   308→                borderColor: "#dc2626",
   309→                color: "#dc2626",
   310→              }}
   311→            >
   312→              <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   313→              Delete
   314→            </button>
   315→            <button
   316→              onClick={handleTriggerRun}
   317→              disabled={triggering}
   318→              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] font-medium text-white transition-colors disabled:opacity-60"
   319→              style={{
   320→                fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif",
   321→                backgroundColor: "#e8601a",
   322→              }}
   323→            >
   324→              <Play className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   325→              {triggering ? "Running..." : "Run Agent"}
   326→            </button>
   327→          </div>
   328→        </div>
   329→      </div>
   330→
   331→      {/* ===== Tab bar ===== */}
   332→      <div
   333→        className="-mx-6 border-b px-6"
   334→        style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#ffffff" }}
   335→      >
   336→        <div className="flex gap-0">
   337→          {tabs.map((tab) => {
   338→            const isActive = activeTab === tab.key;
   339→            return (
   340→              <button
   341→                key={tab.key}
   342→                onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.key)}
   343→                className="relative px-4 py-2.5 text-[13px] font-medium transition-colors"
   344→                style={{
   345→                  fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif",
   346→                  color: isActive ? "#111113" : "#9ca3af",
   347→                }}
   348→              >
   349→                {tab.label}
   350→                {isActive && (
   351→                  <span
   352→                    className="absolute bottom-0 left-4 right-4 h-[2px]"
   353→                    style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a" }}
   354→                  />
   355→                )}
   356→              </button>
   357→            );
   358→          })}
   359→        </div>
   360→      </div>
   361→
   362→      {/* ===== Tab content ===== */}
   363→      <div className="pt-6">
   364→        {/* --- Sources tab --- */}
   365→        {activeTab === "sources" && (
   366→          <div>
   367→            <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
   368→              <SectionLabel>Sources</SectionLabel>
   369→              <button
   370→                onClick={() => setSourceCreateOpen(true)}
   371→                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] font-medium transition-colors hover:bg-[#f3f3f1]"
   372→                style={{
   373→                  fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif",
   374→                  borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   375→                  color: "#374151",
   376→                }}
   377→              >
   378→                <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   379→                Add Source
   380→              </button>
   381→            </div>
   382→
   383→            {sourcesData?.data && sourcesData.data.length > 0 ? (
   384→              <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-md border" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   385→                <table className="w-full text-[13px]" style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif" }}>
   386→                  <thead>
   387→                    <tr style={{ backgroundColor: "#fafaf9" }}>
   388→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Provider</th>
   389→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Repository</th>
   390→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Interval</th>
   391→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Status</th>
   392→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Last polled</th>
   393→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-right text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}></th>
   394→                    </tr>
   395→                  </thead>
   396→                  <tbody>
   397→                    {sourcesData.data.map((source) => (
   398→                      <tr key={source.id} className="border-t" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   399→                        <td className="px-4 py-3">
   400→                          <ProviderBadge provider={source.provider} />
   401→                        </td>
   402→                        <td className="px-4 py-3">
   403→                          <span style={{ fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", fontSize: "12px" }}>
   404→                            {source.repository}
   405→                          </span>
   406→                        </td>
   407→                        <td className="px-4 py-3" style={{ color: "#6b7280" }}>
   408→                          {formatInterval(source.poll_interval_seconds)}
   409→                        </td>
   410→                        <td className="px-4 py-3">
   411→                          <button
   412→                            onClick={() => handleToggleSource(source)}
   413→                            className="relative inline-flex h-5 w-9 shrink-0 cursor-pointer items-center rounded-full transition-colors duration-200"
   414→                            style={{ backgroundColor: source.enabled ? "#16a34a" : "#d1d5db" }}
   415→                            title={source.enabled ? "Disable polling" : "Enable polling"}
   416→                          >
   417→                            <span
   418→                              className="inline-block h-3.5 w-3.5 rounded-full bg-white shadow transition-transform duration-200"
   419→                              style={{ transform: source.enabled ? "translateX(18px)" : "translateX(3px)" }}
   420→                            />
   421→                          </button>
   422→                        </td>
   423→                        <td className="px-4 py-3" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   424→                          {source.last_polled_at
   425→                            ? new Date(source.last_polled_at).toLocaleString()
   426→                            : "Never"}
   427→                        </td>
   428→                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right">
   429→                          <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
   430→                            <button
   431→                              onClick={() => setEditingSource(source)}
   432→                              className="rounded p-1 text-[#9ca3af] transition-colors hover:bg-[#f3f3f1] hover:text-[#111113]"
   433→                              title="Edit source"
   434→                            >
   435→                              <Pencil className="h-4 w-4" />
   436→                            </button>
   437→                            <button
   438→                              onClick={() => setDeletingSourceId(source.id)}
   439→                              className="rounded p-1 text-[#9ca3af] transition-colors hover:bg-red-50 hover:text-red-600"
   440→                              title="Delete source"
   441→                            >
   442→                              <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
   443→                            </button>
   444→                          </div>
   445→                        </td>
   446→                      </tr>
   447→                    ))}
   448→                  </tbody>
   449→                </table>
   450→              </div>
   451→            ) : (
   452→              <div
   453→                className="flex h-32 items-center justify-center rounded-md border text-[13px]"
   454→                style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", color: "#9ca3af" }}
   455→              >
   456→                No sources configured
   457→              </div>
   458→            )}
   459→          </div>
   460→        )}
   461→
   462→        {/* --- Context Sources tab --- */}
   463→        {activeTab === "context" && (
   464→          <div>
   465→            <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
   466→              <SectionLabel>Context Sources</SectionLabel>
   467→              <button
   468→                onClick={() => setCtxCreateOpen(true)}
   469→                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] font-medium transition-colors hover:bg-[#f3f3f1]"
   470→                style={{
   471→                  fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif",
   472→                  borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   473→                  color: "#374151",
   474→                }}
   475→              >
   476→                <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   477→                Add Context Source
   478→              </button>
   479→            </div>
   480→
   481→            {ctxData?.data && ctxData.data.length > 0 ? (
   482→              <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-md border" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   483→                <table className="w-full text-[13px]" style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif" }}>
   484→                  <thead>
   485→                    <tr style={{ backgroundColor: "#fafaf9" }}>
   486→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Type</th>
   487→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Name</th>
   488→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Config URL</th>
   489→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-right text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}></th>
   490→                    </tr>
   491→                  </thead>
   492→                  <tbody>
   493→                    {ctxData.data.map((ctx) => (
   494→                      <tr key={ctx.id} className="border-t" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   495→                        <td className="px-4 py-3">
   496→                          <span
   497→                            className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-medium leading-none"
   498→                            style={{ backgroundColor: "#f3f3f1", color: "#374151" }}
   499→                          >
   500→                            {ctx.type}
   501→                          </span>
   502→                        </td>
   503→                        <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium" style={{ color: "#111113" }}>
   504→                          {ctx.name}
   505→                        </td>
   506→                        <td className="px-4 py-3" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   507→                          <span
   508→                            className="text-[12px]"
   509→                            style={{ fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace" }}
   510→                            title={typeof ctx.config?.url === "string" ? ctx.config.url : ""}
   511→                          >
   512→                            {typeof ctx.config?.url === "string"
   513→                              ? truncate(ctx.config.url, 50)
   514→                              : "--"}
   515→                          </span>
   516→                        </td>
   517→                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right">
   518→                          <button
   519→                            onClick={() => setDeletingCtxId(ctx.id)}
   520→                            className="rounded p-1 text-[#9ca3af] transition-colors hover:bg-red-50 hover:text-red-600"
   521→                            title="Delete context source"
   522→                          >
   523→                            <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
   524→                          </button>
   525→                        </td>
   526→                      </tr>
   527→                    ))}
   528→                  </tbody>
   529→                </table>
   530→              </div>
   531→            ) : (
   532→              <div
   533→                className="flex h-32 items-center justify-center rounded-md border text-[13px]"
   534→                style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", color: "#9ca3af" }}
   535→              >
   536→                No context sources configured
   537→              </div>
   538→            )}
   539→          </div>
   540→        )}
   541→
   542→        {/* --- Agent tab --- */}
   543→        {activeTab === "agent" && (
   544→          <div className="space-y-8">
   545→            {/* Prompt section */}
   546→            <div>
   547→              <SectionLabel className="mb-3">Agent Prompt</SectionLabel>
   548→              <textarea
   549→                value={currentPrompt}
   550→                onChange={(e) => setPromptDraft(e.target.value)}
   551→                rows={5}
   552→                placeholder="Custom instructions for the agent when analyzing releases..."
   553→                className="w-full resize-y rounded-md border px-3 py-2 text-[13px] placeholder:text-[#9ca3af] focus:outline-none focus:ring-1"
   554→                style={{
   555→                  fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif",
   556→                  backgroundColor: "#fafaf9",
   557→                  borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   558→                  color: "#111113",
   559→                }}
   560→              />
   561→              <div className="mt-2 flex justify-end">
   562→                <button
   563→                  onClick={handleSaveAgentConfig}
   564→                  disabled={saving || (promptDraft === null && rulesDraft === null)}
   565→                  className="rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] font-medium text-white transition-colors disabled:opacity-40"
   566→                  style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a" }}
   567→                >
   568→                  {saving ? "Saving..." : "Save"}
   569→                </button>
   570→              </div>
   571→            </div>
   572→
   573→            {/* Rules section */}
   574→            <div>
   575→              <SectionLabel className="mb-3">Trigger Rules</SectionLabel>
   576→              <div className="space-y-3">
   577→                <label className="flex items-center gap-2.5 text-[13px]" style={{ color: "#374151" }}>
   578→                  <input
   579→                    type="checkbox"
   580→                    checked={currentRules.on_major_release ?? false}
   581→                    onChange={(e) =>
   582→                      setRulesDraft({ ...currentRules, on_major_release: e.target.checked })
   583→                    }
   584→                    className="h-4 w-4 rounded border accent-[#e8601a]"
   585→                    style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}
   586→                  />
   587→                  On Major Release
   588→                </label>
   589→                <label className="flex items-center gap-2.5 text-[13px]" style={{ color: "#374151" }}>
   590→                  <input
   591→                    type="checkbox"
   592→                    checked={currentRules.on_minor_release ?? false}
   593→                    onChange={(e) =>
   594→                      setRulesDraft({ ...currentRules, on_minor_release: e.target.checked })
   595→                    }

> AGENT

I can see the issue. The SWR calls for sources and context sources data are **conditional on the active tab** (lines 86-93):

- `sourcesData` only fetches when `activeTab === "sources"`
- `ctxData` only fetches when `activeTab === "context"`

So at lines 295-297 in the header, `sourceCount` and `ctxCount` are derived from these conditional fetches. When you're on the "Sources" tab, `ctxData` is `undefined` (never fetched), so it shows `"-- context sources"`. When you switch to the "Context Sources" tab, `sourcesData` becomes `undefined`, so it shows `"-- sources"`.

The fix is to always fetch both counts regardless of the active tab.

  "old_string": "  const { data: sourcesData, mutate: mutateSources } = useSWR(\n    activeTab === \"sources\" ? `project-${id}-sources` : null,\n    () => sourcesApi.listByProject(id),\n  );\n  const { data: ctxData, mutate: mutateCtx } = useSWR(\n    activeTab === \"context\" ? `project-${id}-ctx` : null,\n    () => ctxApi.list(id),\n  );",
  "new_string": "  const { data: sourcesData, mutate: mutateSources } = useSWR(\n    `project-${id}-sources`,\n    () => sourcesApi.listByProject(id),\n  );\n  const { data: ctxData, mutate: mutateCtx } = useSWR(\n    `project-${id}-ctx`,\n    () => ctxApi.list(id),\n  );"
}

> AGENT

The SWR calls for sources and context sources are now unconditional — they'll fetch regardless of which tab is active. This means `sourceCount` and `ctxCount` will always have values, so the header will show e.g. "1 sources · 2 context sources" instead of "-- context sources" when on the Sources tab.

+func (s *PgStore) ListAllSemanticReleases(ctx context.Context, page, perPage int) ([]models.SemanticRelease, int, error) {
+	var total int
+	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM semantic_releases`).Scan(&total)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("count semantic releases: %w", err)
+	}
+	offset := (page - 1) * perPage
+	rows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx,
+		`SELECT id, project_id, version, COALESCE(report,'{}'), status, COALESCE(error,''),
+		        created_at, completed_at
+		 FROM semantic_releases ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT $1 OFFSET $2`, perPage, offset)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("list all semantic releases: %w", err)
+	}
+	defer rows.Close()
+	var releases []models.SemanticRelease
+	for rows.Next() {
+		var sr models.SemanticRelease
+		if err := rows.Scan(&sr.ID, &sr.ProjectID, &sr.Version, &sr.Report, &sr.Status, &sr.Error,
+			&sr.CreatedAt, &sr.CompletedAt); err != nil {
+			return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("scan semantic release: %w", err)
+		}
+		releases = append(releases, sr)
+	}
+	return releases, total, nil
+}
